### [1] [Chiral Phonons Protected by Symmetry in a Strongly Anharmonic Helical Material](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25089)
*Supriyo Ghosh,Dipti Jasrasaria*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过从头算多体方法研究强非谐螺旋晶体碲（Te），揭示旋转对称性施加的赝角动量选择定则限制了声子-声子相互作用，从而保护手性声子的圆偏振在高温下得以维持，但其寿命仍急剧下降；热梯度产生的角动量温度依赖性由寿命而非手性主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性声子如何在有限温度下经受住非谐声子-声子相互作用的破坏仍不清楚，而后者是有限温度晶格动力学的基础，因此需要厘清手性声子在高温下的存活机制。

Method: 采用从头算多体方法研究强非谐螺旋晶体碲（Te），利用其旋转对称性推导出声子-声子相互作用上的赝角动量选择定则，分析布里渊区旋转不变对称点处的模式混合与散射限制，并考察圆偏振、寿命以及热梯度驱动角动量的温度依赖性。

Result: 旋转对称性在Te中施加的赝角动量选择定则限制了模式混合与散射，保护了振动模式在有限温度下的圆偏振（手性）直至600 K，但并未保护其寿命（寿命急剧下降）；热梯度产生的角动量在10至600 K之间下降了两个数量级，且其温度依赖性由寿命而非手性主导。

Conclusion: 手性声子的动态手性在有限温度下因旋转对称性选择定则而得以保持（至600 K），但寿命受非谐相互作用强烈抑制；角动量随温度的变化主要受寿命控制，而非手性本身。

Abstract: How chiral phonons survive anharmonic phonon-phonon interactions, which underpin lattice dynamics at finite temperature, remains poorly understood. Using an ab initio many-body approach, we study crystalline tellurium (Te), a strongly anharmonic helical crystal. We show that rotational symmetries in Te impose pseudoangular momentum selection rules on phonon-phonon interactions, restricting mode mixing and scattering at rotationally invariant symmetry points in the Brillouin zone. These symmetries protect the circular polarization of vibrational modes at finite temperature but not their lifetimes such that the dynamic chirality of handed vibrational modes persists to 600 K, even as lifetimes drop sharply. We find that the temperature-dependence of angular momentum generated by a thermal gradient is governed by lifetimes rather than chirality, decreasing by two orders of magnitude between 10 and 600 K.

</details>


### [2] [A plane-wave auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo study of silicon point defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25121)
*Moritz Humer,Faruk Salihbegovic,Martin Schlipf,Zoran Sukurma,Georg Kresse*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文用辅助场量子蒙特卡洛（AFQMC）计算硅自间隙原子和空位的形成能，发现经RPA修正后与DMC及transcorrelated耦合簇结果一致，并指出此前CCSD(T)对四面体间隙的高估源于有限尺寸处理而非多组态特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确预测点缺陷能量学是计算材料科学的难题，以四面体硅自间隙为例，周期性CCSD(T)与DMC结果分歧高达约1.2 eV，亟需厘清分歧根源并给出可靠的形成能。

Method: 采用平面波基组、投影缀加波框架下的辅助场量子蒙特卡洛（AFQMC），并与随机相近似（RPA）和CCSD(T)对比；使用16原子和64原子超胞，利用基于RPA的修正消除AFQMC扭转平均后残留的二体有限尺寸误差。

Result: 在16原子胞中，哑铃型和六方型自间隙形成能为4.4 eV和4.6 eV，与DMC及transcorrelated耦合簇结果一致；四面体间隙和Jahn-Teller畸变空位需64原子胞，分别得到5.7 eV和5.2 eV。相同设置下16原子胞中CCSD(T)形成能系统性偏低，四面体间隙差异最大达250 meV。

Conclusion: 此前CCSD(T)对四面体间隙的高估源于耦合簇工作流中的有限尺寸处理，而非缺陷的多组态特性。RPA修正可有效消除AFQMC的有限尺寸误差，使结果与DMC及transcorrelated耦合簇方法吻合。

Abstract: Accurately predicting the energetics of point defects remains a formidable challenge in computational materials science. For the tetrahedral silicon self-interstitial, for example, previous periodic coupled-cluster [CCSD(T)] and diffusion Monte Carlo (DMC) calculations disagree by about 1.2 eV. Here, we present formation energies of silicon self-interstitials and vacancies using auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo (AFQMC) in a plane-wave basis formulated within the projector-augmented wave framework, and compare them with the random-phase approximation (RPA) and CCSD(T). Using 16- and 64-atom cells, we demonstrate that an RPA-based correction effectively removes the two-body finite-size errors that remain after twist averaging in AFQMC for bulk silicon and for the dumbbell and hexagonal self-interstitials. With this correction, we obtain formation energies of 4.4 eV and 4.6 eV for these two self-interstitials in 16-atom cells, in good agreement with DMC and transcorrelated coupled-cluster results. For the tetrahedral interstitial and the Jahn--Teller-distorted vacancy, 16-atom cells are too small to obtain realistic formation energies; in 64-atom cells, we obtain 5.7 eV and 5.2 eV, respectively. With identical setups in 16-atom cells, CCSD(T) yields systematically lower formation energies than AFQMC, with the largest difference, 250 meV, for the tetrahedral interstitial. Our results indicate that the earlier overestimation of CCSD(T) for the tetrahedral interstitial stems from the finite-size treatment in the coupled-cluster workflow rather than from multi-configurational character of the defect.

</details>


### [3] [Machine Learning-Driven High-Throughput Screening of Equimolar and Non-Equimolar High-Entropy Carbides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25127)
*Arina A. Svinukhova,Faisal Baber,Michel M. Lukanov,Viktor S. Baidyshev,Daria Terentieva,Alexander Ya. Pak,Alexander G. Kvashnin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出利用机器学习原子间势（MACE 与 DeePMD）对 IVB/VB 族过渡金属（Ti、Zr、Hf、V、Nb、Ta）高熵碳化物进行高通量筛选，克服了实验和从头计算难以覆盖庞大成分空间的问题，并预测出一批等摩尔与非等摩尔的稳定成分以供实验合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高熵陶瓷最优成分的搜索面临巨大的成分空间，既无法用实验穷举，也无法靠从头计算（ab initio）大规模测试，因此亟需高效、可扩展的筛选手段。

Method: 采用基于从头计算数据集训练的大原子模型 Machine-learned Atomic Cluster Expansion（MACE）和深度学习势 DeePMD（Deep Potential Molecular Dynamics），对 Ti、Zr、Hf、V、Nb、Ta 六个过渡金属的碳化物中数千种潜在的非等摩尔（以及等摩尔）成分进行高通量稳定性筛选。

Result: 筛选预测出一组等摩尔与非等摩尔的高熵碳化物（HEC）稳定成分，为后续按预测结果进行实验合成提供了候选。

Conclusion: 机器学习原子间势可有效地在高熵碳化物的庞大成分空间中进行高通量筛选，并成功预测出可进一步实验合成的稳定 HEC 成分，为高熵陶瓷成分设计提供了可行路线。

Abstract: Search for optimal compositions among high-entropy ceramics are challenging due to large composition space, which are unable to test either experimentally or by \textit{ab initio} calculations. Here we present a high-throughput screening for equimolar and non-equimolar high-entropy carbides (HEC) among group IVB and VB transition metals (Ti, Zr, Hf, V, Nb and Ta). Large Atomistic Model MACE, and deep learning based interatomic potential DeePMD trained on \textit{ab initio}-based dataset of structures are used to effectively screen over the thousands of potential non-equimolar compositions among high-entropy carbides of Ti, Zr, Hf, V, Nb and Ta transition metals. A set of both equimolar and non-equimolar HECs are predicted to be stable in order to further synthesize those compositions based on the predictions.

</details>


### [4] [A Symmetry-Stratified Framework for Phase Transitions: Thermodynamic States, Critical Dynamics, and Spectral Mechanical Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25212)
*Pengfei Zhu,Leiming Du*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一个对称性分层框架，通过有限维内禀状态与母相不变自由能，将结构对称性、热力学相稳定性和弹性波响应统一起来，并给出声学简并如何劈裂及其标度律的判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构对称性、热力学相稳定性与弹性波响应虽相互关联，但对相变固体的刻画方式各异，缺乏统一的连接框架，导致难以从力学谱推断结构变化。

Method: 建立对称性分层框架：以稳定子群分类结构态，以自由能比较与稳定性条件确定平衡支与耗散演化；利用等变性使冻结弹性切线继承结构稳定子；借助小群表示与简并微扰论判定声学双重态的劈裂及其最低非零劈裂阶 p；在序参量指数为 β 的连续支上，推导可观测声学能隙按 |t|^{pβ} 标度；并通过张量实现与立方-四方相变实例给出线性、二次劈裂通道和方向性保护简并，辅以自由能重构、共存、弹性相容性、有限温谱和受控临界读数等数值基准及 NiTi 测量再分析。

Result: 推导出在选定的连续支上，规则可观测声学能隙以 |t|^{pβ} 标度（前提是临界自能与结构弛豫不主导测量）；明确了应要求等变性之外的额外可辨识条件；给出线性与二次劈裂通道及方向性保护简并的具体实现；NiTi 再分析揭示了有效弹性简并、热历史依赖性，以及独立测量的相分数具有预测价值。

Conclusion: 该框架明确了从力学谱推断结构变化所需的构性假设与测量假设，为把弹性波观测与相变结构、热力学性质定量关联提供了统一理论工具。

Abstract: Structural symmetry, thermodynamic phase stability, and elastic-wave response provide related but distinct descriptions of a transforming solid. We formulate a symmetry-stratified framework that connects these descriptions through a finite-dimensional internal state and a parent-invariant free energy. Stabilizers classify structural states, whereas free-energy comparisons and stability conditions determine equilibrium branches and dissipative evolution. Equivariance ensures that the frozen elastic tangent inherits the structural stabilizer. Equality requires additional identifiability conditions. Little-group representations and degenerate perturbation theory then determine whether an acoustic doublet splits and the first nonzero splitting order $p$. On a selected continuous branch with order-parameter exponent $β$, a regular, observable acoustic gap scales as $|t|^{pβ}$, provided critical self-energies and structural relaxation do not dominate the measurement. Explicit tensorial and cubic-to-tetragonal realizations yield linear and quadratic splitting channels, together with directionally protected degeneracies. Separate numerical benchmarks examine free-energy reconstruction, coexistence, elastic compatibility, finite-temperature spectra, and controlled critical readouts. Reanalysis of NiTi measurements illustrates effective elastic degeneracy, thermal-history dependence, and the predictive value of independently measured phase fractions. Most importantly, the framework identifies the constitutive and measurement assumptions required to infer structural changes from mechanical spectra.

</details>


### [5] [Single-Crystal Growth, Structure, and Physical Properties of Yb2MoO6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25225)
*Ali Sarikhani,Santhoshkumar Sundaramoorthy,Sadracd Gamez,Mathew Pollard,Deepak Singh,Jeremy Watts,Steven Kelley,Amitava Choudhury,Yew San Hor*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文成功合成了毫米级 Yb2MoO6 单晶，通过单晶 X 射线衍射确定其结构，并表征了其光学与磁学性质，为这一稀土钼酸盐功能氧化物的后续研究奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土钼酸盐因其结构和化学上的灵活性而展现出多样的磁、电、光学行为，具有重要的功能氧化物研究价值；然而 Yb2MoO6 的单晶生长及其物理性质的系统研究尚不充分，制约了对该材料潜在应用的探索。

Method: 合成了毫米级 Yb2MoO6 单晶，采用单晶 X 射线衍射确定晶体结构并获得晶格参数；利用扫描电子显微镜和能量色散谱验证 Yb:Mo 化学计量比；通过漫反射光谱结合 Tauc 分析估算光学带隙；通过磁化率测量研究磁学行为，并用居里-外斯定律拟合。

Result: 单晶 X 射线衍射确认形成 Yb2MoO6，晶格参数 a=16.228(2) Å、b=10.901(3) Å、c=5.286(2) Å，与先前中子粉末衍射数据一致；SEM/EDS 显示 Yb:Mo 比接近 2:1；在直接允许跃迁假设下估算光学带隙约 3.28 eV；磁化率呈现类居里-外斯顺磁行为，有效磁矩 μeff≈4.2 μB/Yb，外斯温度 θ≈-65.7 K，暗示可能存在反铁磁相互作用或关联，降温至 10 K 未观察到明显的磁有序转变。

Conclusion: 成功建立 Yb2MoO6 单晶的生长，并对其结构、光学和磁学性质提供了初步认知，为深入研究该稀土钼酸盐的功能特性创造了条件。

Abstract: Rare-earth molybdates are functional oxides of considerable interest because their structural and chemical flexibility enables diverse magnetic, electronic, and optical behavior. Here, millimeter-sized single crystals of $Yb_2MoO_6$ are synthesized, their crystal structure determined and physical properties investigated. Single-crystal X-ray diffraction confirms the formation of $Yb_2MoO_6$ and yielded lattice parameters of $a = 16.228(2)$ $\mathring{A}$, $b = 10.901(3)$ $\mathring{A}$, and $c = 5.286(2)$ $\mathring{A}$, in good agreement with previously reported neutron powder diffraction data. Scanning electron microscopy and energy-dispersive spectroscopy show a Yb:Mo ratio close to the expected 2:1 stoichiometry. Optical measurements based on diffuse-reflectance spectra and Tauc analysis yield an estimated optical gap of $\sim 3.28$ eV under the direct-allowed transition assumption. Magnetic susceptibility measurements reveal Curie-Weiss-like paramagnetic behavior, with an effective magnetic moment of $μ_{\mathrm{eff}} \approx 4.2μ_B$ per Yb and an apparent Weiss temperature of $θ\approx -65.7$ K, consistent with possible antiferromagnetic interactions or correlations, with no clear magnetic-ordering transition observed down to 10 K. These results establish the growth of $Yb_2MoO_6$ single crystals and provide initial insight into their structural, optical, and magnetic properties.

</details>


### [6] [Ab-initio study of magnetic interaction in RNiO2: role of rare-earth size and epitaxial strain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25907)
*Ramasamy Murugesan,Shiyu Deng,Marie-Bernadette Lepetit*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理系统研究了无限层 RNiO2（R=La-Lu）薄膜在衬底约束下的结构与最近邻磁交换相互作用，揭示稀土离子半径和外延应变对磁相互作用的调控作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限层镍酸盐的结构畸变与衬底诱导应变对磁交换相互作用的影响尚缺乏系统的定量认识，需在实验（如RIXS）可比的精度下厘清其调控规律。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT）优化晶体结构，结合CAS+DDCI关联波函数方法计算最近邻磁交换相互作用，并覆盖稀土元素系列（R=La-Lu）及不同衬底约束。

Result: 除La和Ce外，RNiO2采用I4/mcm空间群，具有NiO4平面外旋转畸变；衬底约束下该结论仍成立，压缩应变减小旋转畸变幅度并增强磁交换相互作用；磁交换对稀土离子半径和外延应变均高度敏感。

Conclusion: 结构畸变与衬底诱导应变在调控无限层镍酸盐磁相互作用中起关键作用，所提方法可给出与RIXS实验一致的结果。

Abstract: In this paper we present a systematic first-principles investigation of the structure and nearest-neighbor magnetic exchange interactions in the infinite-layer RNiO2 thin-film series (R = La-Lu), under the constrain of different substrates. Density functional theory calculations are first employed to optimize the crystal structures, while CAS+DDCI correlated wave-function calculations were used for the determination of the magnetic exchanges. This methodology allows to obtain magnetic interactions in good agreement with RIXS experimental estimations. In the pristine case, our results confirm that the structure of RNiO2 (except for R = La and Ce) adopts the I4/mcm space group, characterized by an out-of-plane rotation of the NiO4 plaquettes. This result remains valid under substrate constrains, even if compressive strain reduces the amplitude of the rotational distortion and leads to an enhancement of the magnetic exchange interaction relative to the pristine structures. The latter are found to depend sensitively on both the rare-earth ionic radius and the applied epitaxial strain. These results highlight the role of structural distortions and substrate-induced strain in tuning magnetic interactions in infinite-layer nickelates.

</details>


### [7] [Molecular engineering for crack-free transfer of single-crystalline oxide membranes onto silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25246)
*Jeongkeun Song,Jihye Choi,Benjamin Doughty,Young-Hoon Kim,Keun-Yeol Park,Uvinduni I. Premadasa,Miaofang Chi,Seung Sae Hong,Celesta S. Chang,Zheng Gai,Ambrose Seo,Tomonori Saito,Ho Nyung Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 作者提出一种界面能控制的释放策略，通过降低支撑聚合物聚碳酸丙烯酯的分子量（聚合物解构）来提高其极性和表面能，从而抑制界面起泡，制备出厘米级、无裂纹的单晶自支撑氧化物薄膜，并成功拓展到La0.7Sr0.3MnO3，在Si上保留了铁磁性和金属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将单晶复杂氧化物与硅异质集成，是实现下一代微电子器件功能集成的关键途径。自支撑氧化物薄膜（从生长衬底上释放）是实现该集成的可行方式，但释放和转移过程中出现的裂纹、褶皱和碎裂严重限制了薄膜的尺寸与质量，亟待解决。

Method: 以SrTiO3为模型体系，通过时间分辨释放研究，发现长时间水暴露下膜-支撑界面处水诱导的起泡是薄膜开裂的根源。提出界面能控制的释放策略：利用聚合物解构降低聚碳酸丙烯酯（PPC）支撑层的分子量，提高其极性和表面能，从而抑制界面起泡，并减少支撑层去除后的有机残留。

Result: 获得了厘米级横向尺寸、无裂纹的单晶自支撑SrTiO3薄膜，其光学性质与块体单晶相当。该方法还推广到金属性铁磁体La0.7Sr0.3MnO3，在Si上得到无裂纹薄膜，且保留了铁磁性和金属性。

Conclusion: 界面能控制的释放策略有效解决了自支撑氧化物薄膜释放与转移中的开裂问题，能够实现大尺寸、高质量、无裂纹的单晶氧化物薄膜，并通用地适用于多种功能氧化物，为氧化物与硅的异质集成开辟了可行路径。

Abstract: Heterogeneous integration of single-crystalline complex oxides with silicon offers a route to incorporating their functionalities into next-generation microelectronics. Released from their growth substrates, freestanding oxide membranes enable this integration. However, cracks, wrinkles, and fragmentation during release and transfer limit membrane size and quality. Here, we present an interfacial energy-controlled release strategy that enables crack-free, single-crystalline freestanding oxide membranes with centimeter-scale lateral dimensions. Using SrTiO3 as a model system, time-resolved release studies identify water-induced blistering at the membrane-support interface during prolonged water exposure as the origin of membrane cracking. Lowering the molecular weight of the poly(propylene carbonate) support through polymer deconstruction increases its polarity and surface energy, suppresses interfacial blistering, and reduces organic residue after support removal. The resulting SrTiO3 membranes exhibit optical properties comparable to those of bulk single crystals. We extend this approach to the metallic ferromagnet La0.7Sr0.3MnO3, obtaining crack-free membranes on Si that preserve ferromagnetism and metallicity.

</details>


### [8] [Altermagnetic magnon splitting in hematite: from bulk g-wave to planar i-wave](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25284)
*Tobias Dannegger,Julian A. Beisch,Levente Rózsa,László Szunyogh,Ulrich Nowak*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过完全相对论第一性原理计算研究了赤铁矿（hematite）的磁振子性质，重点分析交变磁（altermagnetic）效应。发现偶极相互作用导致非交变磁分裂和相反手性磁振子杂化，且在k_z=0平面频率与寿命呈现显著交变磁分裂；通过对比MnTe揭示了三角交变磁体同时具有体g波与面内i波分量，点对称性不足以完全描述分裂函数的对称性行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Metal halide perovskites have spin-orbit coupling among the strongest in semiconductors, yet whether their centrosymmetric bulk phases can convert charge current into spin current, and what controls the response, is unknown. Because the valence band maximum is an s-p singlet, shifted only in second order, these contributions cancel in the undoped compounds, which respond like conventional semiconductors. With hole doping the response recovers, and at densities reachable by electric double-layer gating CsPbI3 attains the spin Hall conductivity of p-GaAs. The response is therefore determined by the position of the halide-p bands relative to the valence band maximum, not by the atomic spin-orbit constant, a criterion that applies to any semiconductor with an orbital-singlet band edge. These results point to CsPbI3 as a candidate gate-controlled source of spin current for perovskite spin-orbit torque devices.

Among them, the anisotropic magnetic dipole (AMD), which reflects the local orbital anisotropy, shares the symmetry of magnetization and can have a ferroic component even when the spin moments cancel. We show that the staggered rotation of the FeO$_6$ octahedra gives rise to ferroic AMD components despite the antiparallel spin alignment. As the local magnetic symmetry varies with the cycloidal phase, these AMDs are spatially modulated and produce the dichroism through interference with the sinusoidal spin component. Our findings demonstrate that long-wavelength antiferromagnetic textures can host spatially modulated AMDs, opening new avenues for exploring hidden multipole textures in magnetic and multiferroic materials.

Abstract: Fast and accurate prediction of adsorption energies is critical for high-throughput computational catalyst screening, existing methods compromise either on evaluation speed (full-graph machine learning potentials) or geometric sensitivity (coordination-based models like the alpha-scheme). Here, we present AdsNet, an equivariant graph neural network that directly predicts adsorption energies in a single forward pass, which operates on a local graph restricted only to adsorbate atoms and its immediate surface neighbors within a cutoff radius. This local graph formulation decouples computational complexity from total system size while encoding continuous geometric features (interatomic distances and directional vectors) enabling sensitivity to subtle variations in local bonding environments. AdsNet adopts a message-passing architecture with scalar and vector node features over multiple interaction layers. Trained on AdsTraj, a dataset of 33,920 density functional theory (DFT) calculated structures spanning 26 transition metals and 6 adsorbates (H*, C*, O*, CO*, OH*, HOCO*), the model achieves an adsorption energy root-mean-square error (RMSE) of 0.032 eV and a force RMSE of 0.047 eV/A. Analytic forces obtained via automatic differentiation enable structure relaxation through a projected gradient descent algorithm with adaptive line search, eliminating entire geometry optimizations. Additionally, AdsNet uniquely decomposes predicted adsorption energies into contributions from individual atoms within the local adsorbate graph - an interpretability feature unattainable with conventional machine-learning potentials. AdsNet thus establishes an accurate, highly efficient, and interpretable framework for large-scale adsorption energy prediction and local interface relaxation across various material surfaces.

Abstract: The ability to measure thermal properties and visualize heat transport processes with high spatial resolution is becoming increasingly important for materials discovery, next-generation electronics, and advanced manufacturing. As critical dimensions and architectures of novel materials and high performance electronics shrink and become more heterogeneous, thermal transport is no longer governed by bulk material properties alone, but by local variations in microstructure, such as interfaces and defects, while the operating conditions can also play a role. With the development of non-contact pump-probe thermoreflectance techniques, specific heat, thermal conductivity, and interface conductance can be mapped either through wide-field thermos-reflectance images or by scanning technique. However, key challenges remain such as the balance between imaging throughput and measurement accuracy, the extraction of multiple parameters with high confidence, and non-intrusive subsurface characterization, etc. This Review highlights recent advancements in artificial intelligence (AI)-assistance in high-throughput data processing, visualizing interfacial thermal transport, and nonintrusive subsurface analysis, and offers outlooks for future research directions such as spectral mapping of heat carriers, three-dimensional property mapping, and AI-agentic experimental designs. We anticipate this Review will motivate future efforts in combining AI with experimental techniques to achieve unprecedented capabilities for thermal characterization of nanoscale materials and device characterization, facilitating materials discovery and electronics revolution.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 实验数据与基于标准Drude响应函数的理论预测之间存在定量和定性上的双重分歧，表明在低频s偏振倏逝波区域，Drude响应函数被实验数据排除。由于正是s偏振倏逝波导致了Casimir难题，该结果揭示了Casimir难题的成因。

Abstract: We report a high-precision experiment on measuring the low-frequency magnetic field reflected from a metallic plate. This experiment is designed to give insight into why theoretical predictions of the fundamental Lifshitz theory using the standard response function to the electromagnetic waves are in contradiction with the data of numerous precision measurements of the Casimir force (the Casimir puzzle). To attain these ends, we measured the magnitude and phase of the lateral component of the magnetic field produced by an oscillating magnetic dipole and reflected from a metallic plate. These quantities are completely determined by the evanescent waves with the transverse electric (s) polarization. The obtained measurement data are compared with theoretical predictions using the standard Drude response function of the metal to the low-frequency electromagnetic waves. It is shown that there are both quantitative and qualitative disagreements with these predictions, so that in the region of low-frequency s-polarized evanescent waves the Drude response function is excluded by the data. Keeping in mind that just the s-polarized evanescent waves are responsible for the Casimir puzzle, our results highlight the causes for its emergence. It is emphasized that, in addition to the Casimir effect, searching for the adequate nonlocal response functions of metals to the evanescent waves is of utmost significance for many areas of physics as near-field optical microscopy, nanophotonics, surface plasmon polaritons, radiative heat transfer, and others.

Conclusion: momipy 为动量显微术（含时间分辨）领域提供了一个高效、易用的开源数据分析方案，有助于推动电子能带结构及光诱导动力学的进一步研究。

Abstract: Photoemission momentum microscopy is an emerging approach to photoelectron spectroscopy that is transforming electronic band structure mapping at both laboratory-based and large-scale facilities. Its extension to time-resolved measurements has recently enabled major advances, ranging from the femtosecond imaging of exciton wavefunctions to dark-field photoelectron microscopy of light-induced dynamics and strong light-matter interactions. However, the rapid acquisition of high-dimensional data sets (including electron in-plane momenta $k_x$ and $k_y$, kinetic energy $E_\mathrm{kin}$, time $Δt$, and more) poses substantial challenges for data handling and analysis. A typical workflow encompasses data acquisition, exploratory visualization, calibration and targeted feature extraction, with data management ideally guided by FAIR principles. Here, we present momipy, a Python package that streamlines the momentum microscopy data analysis workflow efficiently and at low computational cost. Among other features, momipy provides interactive 4D data visualization, energy and momentum calibration, space-charge correction, and a broad selection of multidimensional data analysis routines, including binning, momentum-map extraction and time-, energy- and momentum-resolved model fitting. momipy is accompanied by a selection of interactive example scripts, supporting adoption of momipy by both experienced researchers and users new to momentum microscopy.

</details>

EBSD mapping at room temperature reveals cubic austenite in a nonstrained part of the microwire and tetragonal martensite in the strained part. Both EBSD and XRD show that both austenite and martensite have $[001]$ directions approximately parallel to the wire axis. The axial transformation strain calculated from the measured lattice parameters is approximately $15.4\%$. Thermal cycling under constant stresses of $20\text{--}100~\mathrm{MPa}$ reveals experimental transformation strains of $13.2\text{--}14.0\%$. Increasing tensile stress raises the transformation temperatures, with a stress--temperature slope of approximately $1.52~\mathrm{MPa}/^\circ\mathrm{C}$, comparable to that reported for $[001]$-oriented $\mathrm{Ni\text{--}Fe\text{--}Ga}$ single crystals. Overall, the observations confirm that the large macroscopic strain of up to $14.0\%$ relates to the nearly complete martensitic transformation in the microwire.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 第一性原理框架是描述HEMT薄膜层热输运最优的模型，通过先确立本征Umklapp散射主导性质、再逐步叠加散射项的策略，可系统评估并理解模型内在因素对预测能力的影响。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate thermal transport in a set of thin-film layers used in modern HEMT designs. Our theoretical analysis employs three phonon-transport models of increasing sophistication, ranging from a relatively simple Debye-approximation model to a first-principles framework that incorporates Density Functional Theory (DFT)-derived harmonic and anharmonic phonon properties. To describe ternary semiconductors, alloy scattering is also considered, and to account for the properties of non-ideal, polycrystalline thin films, scattering at grain and film boundaries is taken into account. For modeling the specific samples at hand, the latter are fitted to thermal conductivity data measured with Time-Domain Thermoreflectance (TDTR). Overall, we find that the first-principles model performs best and produces the most physically consistent results. Special emphasis is placed on establishing a reliable procedure for determining intrinsic transport properties dominated by Umklapp scattering. Building on these, it is then possible to systematically add additional scattering terms to the models and assess how model-inherent factors, such as the treatment of scattering rates or the description of the phonon dispersion, affect model accuracy and overall predictive capabilities.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多晶 Ni 基合金的晶界（GB）对力学强度和断裂行为影响显著，但 Fe、Cr 等合金元素对晶界内聚力的影响难以用实验和传统 DFT 系统量化；DFT 受计算尺寸限制，难以精细采样合金化构型，因此需要一种既能保证精度又具备足够规模的量化方法。

Result: 在晶界面内以 Cr 或 Fe 取代 Ni 均可提高 W_sep 和 σ_max；所选 MLIP 与 DFT 在这两个量上实现定量一致；合金化引起的晶界内聚力变化同时取决于元素类型和局域结构，展现出 DFT 无法单独揭示的趋势；二元趋势可为解析 Ni-Fe-Cr 三元合金的协同效应提供基础，但显式三元计算才能定量捕捉其相互作用。

Abstract: Grain boundaries (GBs) in polycrystalline Ni-based alloys strongly influence mechanical strength and fracture behavior, yet the effect of alloying elements such as Fe and Cr on GB cohesion remains difficult to quantify systematically. This MLIP enables systematic sampling of alloying configurations at finer concentration steps. Extending the analysis to a ternary $\mathrm{Ni}_{56}\mathrm{Fe}_{20}\mathrm{Cr}_{24}$ composition, inspired by the principal constituents of the widely used commercial Alloy 718, shows that the binary trends provide a useful basis for interpreting the combined effect of both substitutes, while explicit ternary calculations are required to capture their interplay quantitatively. Together, these results provide a validated workflow for quantifying the effect of alloying elements on GB cohesion in multicomponent alloys. The resulting values may serve as input for continuum-scale fracture models, paving the way toward atomistically informed multiscale modeling.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kramers-Weyl 费米子、自旋劈裂费米面、量子几何非线性霍尔与本征自旋霍尔等效应虽各自受到关注，但它们与拓扑表面态、费米弧及手性依赖的量子几何输运之间的共存研究十分匮乏；实现这类显著响应需要合适的对称性、自旋轨道耦合（SOC）与费米面几何的协同，因此需要寻找能同时满足这些条件的真实材料体系。

Abstract: Quantum geometry of Bloch wave functions and crystalline symmetries play a central role in condensed matter physics, shaping the electronic topology and physical responses of solids. In particular, Kramers-Weyl fermions, spin-split Fermi surfaces probed through quantum oscillations and quantum geometric nonlinear Hall and intrinsic spin Hall responses have garnered significant attention. Despite progress in understanding these phenomena individually, their coexistence with topological surface states, Fermi arcs and chirality dependent quantum geometric transport remains scarce, as pronounced responses involve suitable symmetries, SOC and Fermi surface geometry. We observed twelve Kramers-Weyl (KW) crossings with quantized chiral charges $|C|=1$ and $|C|=3$, accompanied by nontrivial surface states and Fermi arcs. The pronounced metallic character gives rise to six Fermi surface sheets exhibiting antisymmetric SOC, with distinct extremal orbits and candidate spin-split pairs resolved through calculated de Haas-van Alphen (dHvA) quantum oscillations. The electronic structure also exhibits large intrinsic Berry curvature dipole (BCD) arising from the asymmetric distribution of Berry curvature, giving rise to a nonlinear Hall response. ### [22] [Lattice dynamics study of (Bi1-xSbx)2Te3 compounds: Comparative compositional study of force constants and Grüneisen parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26010)

Abstract: We performed a comprehensive Raman spectroscopy study of (Bi1-xSbx)2Te3 (BST-x) compounds, with varying Sb concentration, under ambient and high-pressure conditions. At ambient conditions, the frequency of all observed Raman-active modes (E1g, A11g, E2g, A21g) is blue-shifting with increasing Sb concentration, albeit with varying concentration-dependent rate. Based on these results, we calculated the relative force constants as a function of Sb concentration using a harmonic approximation, which shows a mode-dependent variation. The force constants for the lower frequency Raman modes (E1 g and A11g) remain almost composition-independent, while for the higher-frequency ones a profound softening (E2g) or stiffening (A21g) was determined. From the Raman spectroscopy measurements under high-pressure conditions, the corresponding Grüneisen parameters for the three observed Raman-active modes (A11g, E2g, A21g) as a function of composition were determined by combining them with the previously reported Equation of States and the corresponding bulk modulus B0 of the ambient-conditions R-3m phase. The average Grüneisen parameter exhibits a clear maximum for BST-07. Based on Slack's equation, these results are directly related to the optimal ZT values for this composition. Our study provides a solid framework for connecting composition, lattice dynamics, and thermal-transport behaviour, towards establishing the microscopic origin of thermoelectric optimization in BST compounds.

Abstract: Reward misalignment is usually regarded as a failure of artificial intelligence. In materials discovery with generative AI models, however, disagreement between predefined rewards and expert judgment may reveal materials that the rewards overlook. Experts mostly agree with a numerical bandwidth score, but their disagreements are systematic and cannot be explained by an estimated 5% rate of mistaken labels. The learned preferences and numerical score agree on the flattest bands but diverge elsewhere. For example, the preferences favor KCo2As2 and CoGa, reported compounds with partially flat bands that the score would discard. The framework also extends to synthesizability and phonon properties. Our findings suggest that reward misalignment can expose opportunities for materials discovery beyond what fixed rewards are designed to find.

Abstract: This work investigates how defect engineering and nanostructuring via severe plastic deformation influence electrical and thermal transport in the high-entropy alloy CrMnFeCoNi. High-pressure torsion (HPT) for up to N = 4 turns produces a hierarchical defect structure, including deformation twins at low strains, dislocations and Lomer-Cottrell locks at intermediate strains, and a high fraction of high-angle grain boundaries at large strains. While hardness naturally increases and saturates at ~520 Hv, both electrical and thermal conductivity exhibit an unusual increase upon HPT processing (from 0.953 MS m-1 and 10.3 W m-1 K-1 after homogenization to 1.003 +- 0.005 MS m-1 and 14.4 W m-1 K-1 after N = 4). Analysis based on the Wiedemann-Franz law indicates a modest increase in electron transport (~5%) and a significant increase in phonon transport (~100%) after HPT processing. The phonon mean free path remains small on the order of 0.6-1.1 nm, and comparison with the Cahill-Pohl model shows that heat transport occurs near the minimum thermal conductivity limit, where vibrational modes are strongly scattered. This suggests that the enhancement in thermal transport is not governed by conventional defect scattering, but it is hypothesized that modifications in atomic-scale disorder, such as deformation-induced short-range ordering, are responsible for this unusual behavior. Specific heat capacity increases after HPT, consistent with stored defect energy. The linear thermal expansion exhibits a non-monotonic behavior, decreasing initially due to internal stresses and relaxation effects, and increasing at higher strains due to grain boundary-induced anharmonicity. These findings highlight the complex interplay between defects, atomic-scale disorder, and nanostructure in governing the electrical and thermal properties of severely deformed high-entropy alloys via electron and phonon scattering.

Abstract: First-principles structural relaxation and band calculations require substantial computation and expertise. Here we present Perfumer, a large language model-based agentic framework that couples Quantum ESPRESSO with two predictive models. The extracted response in Mn2Au is approximately four times faster than in NiO and substantially faster than in Ni. These observations are consistent with exchange-mediated scattering between antiparallel sublattices enabling rapid loss of antiferromagnetic order without requiring net angular-momentum transfer to an external reservoir, provided sufficient electronic scattering phase space is available. Our results distinguish ultrafast demagnetization from exchange-enhanced precession and identify the interplay of spin-flip probability and electronic scattering rate as a key factor governing the nonequilibrium response of compensated magnets.

Motivation: 基础机器学习原子间势虽在大规模、多样化的DFT数据集上训练，但其验证多基于平衡态，无法保证大规模分子动力学中涌现行为的正确性。作者希望区分静态（热可及）性质与涌现（需外部疲劳应力驱动稀有事件）性质，并通过与物理启发的势函数对比，评估基础模型在疲劳等非平衡场景下的可靠性。

Result: 两种势在许多近平衡性质上与参考数据高度吻合；但NequIP预测的内禀层错能异常偏低（约为EAM的1/7），因此在纳米缺陷疲劳循环下预测出远更多的塑性变形。加载后的每原子能量分布显示，两势在远高于热可及能量的区域差异最大。此外记录了NequIP基础模型的内存相关崩溃与静默外推问题。

Conclusion: 基础机器学习势在平衡态验证良好并不意味着其能正确再现疲劳等驱动稀有事件的涌现行为；层错能等关键能量参数的偏差会显著改变塑性变形预测。作者建议实践者在使用此类基础模型时关注非平衡验证、外推风险及工程稳定性问题。

Abstract: Foundational machine-learned interatomic potentials are trained on large, diverse DFT datasets, but their validation rests on equilibrium states, which does not guarantee correct emergent behavior in large-scale molecular dynamics. Where no direct experimental validation is available, comparisons to physically motivated potentials are valuable for drawing insight. We distinguish static properties, which are thermally accessible, from emergent properties, which require work from an external fatigue stress to drive rare events, and compare the potentials on both. An EAM potential and the NequIP foundational potential both agree closely with reference data for many near-equilibrium properties, but NequIP predicts an anomalously low intrinsic stacking fault energy ($\sim7$ times lower than EAM) and thus predicts far more plastic deformation under fatigue cycling of a nanoscale flaw modeled after the classical Griffith crack problem. Per-atom energy distributions after loading show that the two potentials differ most at energies far above what is thermally accessible. We also document memory-related crashes and silent extrapolation as practical concerns for practitioners of these foundational model forms.

</details>

The key to our construction is introduction of a Landau-dual Chern band, a multi-orbital Chern band obtained from directly sampling the spin-$s$ Dirac Landau level on a Bravais lattice. The exact many-body wavefunctions are given, analytically, in terms of the matrix coordinate $X+iY+J$, where $(X,Y)$ are the lattice coordinates and $J$ is a nilpotent matrix in spin space. The constructed exact pairs cover the fractional Chern states of the Laughlin, the Read-Rezayi, and the Halperin series. Our results prove that FCI ground states exist beyond ideal bands.

From the torque angular dependence in three orthogonal crystallographic planes, we infer a zero-field canted antiferromagnetic structure, with moments almost normal to the honeycomb layers, but weakly canted such as to give rise to a spontaneous magnetization along the in-plane monoclinic $a$-axis, with two magnetic domains related by time reversal. Sharp jumps in the torque angular dependence when the field projection along $a$ changes sign are interpreted as field-induced switching between the two degenerate domains with oppositely aligned ferromagnetic moments. We propose a minimal spin Hamiltonian including easy-axis exchange anisotropy and symmetry-allowed Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya (DM) couplings, for which mean-field calculations reproduce the main features of the observed angle-dependent torque. Torque measurements up to 35 T applied almost normal to the layers observe a strong anomaly attributed to proximity to the field-induced spin-flop transition predicted for the above Hamiltonian. We contrast the predicted magnetic phase diagrams for magnetic fields in different orthogonal crystallographic planes normal to the honeycomb layers and discuss how DM interactions convert some transitions into smooth crossovers and stabilize a phase boundary line connecting the spin-flop transition in field normal to the layers to magnetization saturation for in-plane field normal to $a$; such a phase boundary line does not exist in the absence of DM interactions.

</details>

Abstract: A neural network that learns an inverse map inherits the approximations of the forward solver used to generate its training data. We demonstrate this in the inference of spin Hamiltonians from synthetic neutron-scattering spectra and show how a fast many-body solver can overcome the limitation of linear spin-wave theory (LSWT). We consider a family of anisotropic triangular-lattice spin Hamiltonians at fields just above saturation, where magnon interactions shift and broaden the spectrum in ways that LSWT cannot capture. Using the truncated Hilbert-space ladder (THL) method, we compute the dynamical spin structure factor (DSSF) nonperturbatively within the one- and two-magnon space. THL is fast enough to generate large training sets of DSSF spectra, enabling us to train neural networks that accurately predict Hamiltonian parameters. By empirically benchmarking several neural-network architectures, we find that a one-dimensional convolutional network performs best. A learned forward decoder then reconstructs the DSSF from the predicted parameters, providing a rapid self-consistency check and a visualization of the predicted response. When interactions are strong, networks trained on LSWT and THL spectra infer markedly different parameters. Analysis of the Fisher information matrix shows that the measurement protocol, including which spin-correlation components are resolved and the momentum cut, determines which couplings can be identified. Combining machine learning with fast many- body theory is therefore essential for quantitative Hamiltonian inference from scattering data.

Abstract: We analyze in detail nonstandard statistics for strongly correlated fermions, which comprise the statistical spin liquid (SSL) and model non-Fermi liquid (NFL) cases. The results obtained on a purely physical ground are supplemented with the exact analysis of those coming from microscopic Hubbard-like model in $\mathbf{k}$ space. Specifically, we discuss the transmutation of those statistics to that of localized magnetic moments, as well as the transformation of the Fermi liquid into a strongly correlated fermionic liquid or Mott insulating state; the latter taking place for the half-filled-band situation. The role of exclusion of double occupancies in either real or reciprocal space in the appearance of the correlated phase is stressed. Conclusions concerning the universal features of the models, independence of either the real- or $\mathbf{k}$-space representation, are also discussed. One of those is the boson to fermion statistics transmutation which in the limit of strong repulsive interboson interaction leads to the particle localization instead to the Bose-Einstein condensation.

This mechanism arises from an unusually complex interplay of four different fundamental quasiparticles of condensed matter physics: magnons, optically bright and dark excitons, and phonons. By viewing magnons as the system and the other three quasiparticles as a quantum many-body bath, we derive an extended Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert (eLLG) equation. Its new term generated by the bath is spatially nonlocal and time-dependent, as well as strongly {\em nonlinear} when the {\em crossing of many-body energy levels} of bright and dark excitons occurs via phonon mediation. It also resembles the so-called field-like spin-transfer torque (STT) in spintronics, but STT is driven there with current pulses, so it explains the microscopic origin of the recently experimentally conjectured {\em excitonic STT}. Since dark excitons supply energy to magnons long after the fsLP is switched off, such a reservoir opens avenues for its engineering and applications in optospintronics and optomagnonics, neuromorphic computing, and quantum transduction.

Abstract: A gauge-invariant projector-based expression for the orbital magnetic susceptibility of periodic insulators is obtained from a density-matrix perturbation expansion in a uniform magnetic field. It is shown that, within such an approach, it is natural to distinguish two contributions: the Van Vleck paramagnetic contribution and a geometric contribution. The latter can be expressed in terms of fundamental geometric quantities built from projector derivatives such as the curvature and metric operators, as well as the closely related magnetic moment operator. When applied to two-band and four-band models, the expression for the susceptibility reduces to a simplified formula expressed in terms of the quantum metric. The significance of quantum geometry for the orbital magnetic response is then examined within a lattice model in two dimensions describing the transition between two distinct insulators via a Dirac semimetallic phase.

Abstract: We show that magnon exchange can induce nodal topological superconductivity in a microscopic model of a $p$-wave magnet. Solving the weak-coupling multiband gap equation for itinerant electrons exchange-coupled to localized spins, we find a nodal $p$-wave gap and a critical temperature that rises sharply as the Fermi surface approaches the magnetic Brillouin-zone boundary. In this regime, small-reduced-momentum magnons can scatter electrons between Fermi-surface segments on opposite sides of the zone. These Umklapp processes enhance pairing because the contributions from different sites in the magnetic unit cell cancel less in their electron-magnon vertices than in those for ordinary scattering at comparable reduced momenta. Momentum-resolved winding numbers predict zero-energy Majorana flat bands at symmetry-preserving edges where the bulk nodes project to distinct momenta.

We construct an effective field theory for the resulting dilute vortex gas, coupling the ordinary BKT phase field to an additional field encoding the Majorana-mediated interaction, and show, using renormalization-group and instanton methods, that this hybridization can drive the transition first order, with the metastable phase decaying at an exponentially small rate. Conclusion: 尽管 Tc₃B 的费米面电子态密度 N(EF) 较高，TcB₂ 却因与电子-声子耦合活跃的低频模式匹配最佳而具有最高 Tc，表明这类硼化物的超导排序由电子-声子匹配而非态密度大小决定。

Abstract: Phonon-mediated superconductivity in binary transition-metal borides reflects a balance between the metal d-states, metal-boron bonding, and the lattice vibrations that couple to them. Here, we compare P6/mmm TcB2 at 20 GPa with I4/mcm Tc2B and P4/mmm Tc3B at 0 GPa within a first-principles framework. Their mechanical responses differ appreciably: TcB2 is the most rigid under its compressed condition, Tc2B shows the strongest tendency toward ductility, and Tc3B falls between the two. Despite these differences, all three phases remain metallic. The states at the Fermi level are dominated by Tc-4d character, although the extent of Tc-d/B-p mixing varies from one compound to another. Charge redistribution is more directional and B-centered in TcB2, but more delocalized around the Tc network in Tc2B and Tc3B. Inclusion of spin-orbit coupling leaves all three compounds metallic. The optical response remains metallic and electric field polarization dependent, with substantial visible reflectivity and strong ultraviolet absorption. Low-frequency Tc rich vibrations dominate the electron-phonon coupling (EPC). With μ* = 0.10, the Tc values of TcB2, Tc2B, and Tc3B are 24.92, 11.07, and 15.12 K, respectively. Although Tc3B exhibits a higher electronic density of states at the Fermi level, N(EF), TcB2 couples most strongly to EPC-active low-frequency modes, showing that the superconducting hierarchy depends on electronic-phonon matching rather than DOS magnitude alone.

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个适用于随机反应动力学的通用解析框架，能够统一处理强涨落低拷贝系统的瞬态与稳态行为，为生化网络、生态与流行病学等离散随机种群动力学提供可靠且可扩展的预测工具。

Abstract: Discrete stochastic population dynamics describe systems ranging from biochemical reaction networks and gene regulation to epidemic spreading, ecology, and population genetics, where finite populations generate strong intrinsic fluctuations. Predicting their dynamics, including mean populations, fluctuations, time-lagged correlations, response functions, and steady states, remains challenging: the chemical master equation admits no general analytical solution, numerical solutions scale poorly with system size, and standard approximations fail at low copy numbers. Using a field-theoretic representation of the reaction dynamics, we develop a dynamical variational approximation for stochastic reaction networks based on the Plefka expansion from spin-glass physics. For reaction systems with general stoichiometry and time-dependent rates, the expansion yields tractable non-Markovian effective dynamics with self-consistent memory. We further introduce a generalized self-consistent bubble resummation that provides closed predictions for means, correlations, and response functions. The formulation applies broadly to discrete stochastic population dynamics on networks, and we provide a numerical implementation with adaptive time stepping for user-defined systems. We benchmark the method on stochastic gene regulation, enzyme kinetics, and infection dynamics, where it accurately captures both time-dependent behaviour and steady states, including fluctuation-driven effects missed by conventional approximations. Together, these results establish a general analytical framework for stochastic reaction dynamics across transient and stationary regimes in strongly fluctuating low-copy-number systems.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解长程相互作用（1/r^{1+σ} 衰减）如何改变一阶相变中临界液滴成核的物理图像。在最近邻一维伊辛链中，液滴两侧畴壁的代价与尺寸无关，而在长程相互作用下表面代价依赖于液滴尺寸并与体自由能增益竞争，因此需要重新分析临界半径与激活势垒的标度行为及其对 σ 的依赖。

Method: 采用三种互补方法：(1) 解析分析长程一维伊辛链中两壁表面代价与体自由能增益的竞争，推导临界半径与激活势垒的标度；(2) 建立非局域 φ⁴ 场论，得到具有代数尾巴的临界液滴解，并验证半径与势垒标度；(3) 运用随机长程动力学进行独立数值模拟，复现临界半径标度。

Result: 临界半径标度为 R_c∝h^{-1/σ}（h→0⁺）。σ=1 划分三种动力学区间：σ<1 时激活势垒随 h→0⁺ 发散；σ=1 时势垒呈对数增长；σ>1 时势垒保持有限，与最近邻链行为一致。非局域 φ⁴ 场论给出的临界液滴具有代数衰减的尾部，随机长程动力学独立复现了临界半径标度。

Conclusion: 长程相互作用强度 σ 深刻改变一维系统中一阶相变的亚稳衰变机制：σ=1 是激活势垒行为的分水岭。较低的 σ（更长的相互作用范围）导致临界液滴半径更大、表面代价随尺寸增长更强，使得成核势垒在零场极限下发散；而 σ>1 时恢复与最近邻链类似的有限势垒行为。

Abstract: Metastable decay in first-order phase transitions proceeds through the nucleation of critical droplets. In the one-dimensional nearest-neighbor Ising chain, the cost of the two walls bounding a droplet is independent of its size. For interactions decaying as $1/r^{1+σ}$, this surface cost instead depends on the droplet size and competes with the bulk free-energy gain. The critical radius scales as $R_c\propto h^{-1/σ}$ at small $h$, where $h>0$ is the bias field. The value $σ=1$ separates two regimes: for $σ<1$, the activation barrier diverges as $h\to0^+$; at $σ=1$, it grows logarithmically; and for $σ>1$, it remains finite, as in the nearest-neighbor chain. A nonlocal $φ^4$ field theory yields critical droplets with algebraic tails and confirms the predicted radius and barrier scalings, while stochastic long-range dynamics independently reproduces the critical-radius scaling.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KPZ方程是远离平衡标度不变性的范式，一维已精确求解并被精密实验证实，标度行为也已在二维极化激元凝聚体中观测到；但一维以上尚无任何指数被精确得知，甚至上临界维是否存在都存在争议。

Method: 将KPZ方程的非线性项视为作用于基底某一分形子集上的有效噪声，据此推导粗糙度指数α的上下界，并系统审视这些界背后的假设，与四十年的理论、数值模拟及新实验（特别是二维极化激元实验）相互对照。

Result: 得到d维空间中粗糙度指数的界限为1/(d+1)≤α≤1/d；该界与已有理论、模拟及实验基本一致，并指出了在三维及更高维中最具判别力的检验方式。

Conclusion: 对KPZ粗糙度指数的坚实论断不必依赖精确解；所提出的分形有效噪声图像给出了α的普适界，但需通过高维（尤其是三维及以上）的理论与实验检验来确立或推翻这些界。

Abstract: Forty years after it was written down, the Kardar--Parisi--Zhang (KPZ) equation is the paradigm of scale invariance far from equilibrium. In one dimension it has been solved exactly and confirmed by precision experiments, and its scaling has now been observed in two-dimensional polariton condensates. Above one dimension, however, not a single exponent is known exactly, and even the existence of an upper critical dimension is disputed. In this Perspective we argue that firm statements need not wait for exact solutions. Treating the nonlinearity as an effective noise that acts on a fractal subset of the substrate leads to bounds on the roughness exponent, $1/(d+1)\leα\le 1/d$ in $d$ dimensions. We examine the assumptions behind these bounds, confront them with four decades of theory, simulations and the new experiments, and identify the tests---foremost in three and more dimensions---that would establish or refute them.

</details>

Abstract: In quantum many-body physics spectral statistics is often used as a probe to distinguish integrable and chaotic models. We find a special case of an integrable spin chain with unusual spectral properties. With open boundary conditions the model has homogeneous exponential degeneracies; these are split for periodic boundary conditions. In the latter case, we find that a small number of sectors (selected by a global symmetry) showcase Wigner-Dyson spectral statistics, which usually signals quantum chaos. Our model is clean, translationally invariant, and it fits into the standard Yang-Baxter framework. We identify Hilbert space fragmentation as the key mechanism behind the spectral properties. The fragmentation is non-local, and its extent is different in the case of open and periodic boundary conditions. We argue that the dynamics of the model is not chaotic despite the unusual spectral correlations.

</details>

*Tan Van Vu,Van Tuan Vo,Ruicheng Bao,Keiji Saito*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个基于Doob鞅的框架，为一般马尔可夫过程的可加观测量给出精确的有限时间涨落-响应恒等式，并由此推导出可饱和的热力学不确定关系（TUR）等多种不等式、变分原理与频率域界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间积分可观测量的涨落与响应都可由实验测量，二者之间的关系能揭示维持非平衡态所需的耗散；但缺乏对一般马尔可夫过程在有限时间下精确的涨落-响应恒等式，且传统TUR在速度分辨电流等情形下会失效。

Method: 利用Doob鞅（Doob martingale）将可观测量方差的精确分解为初始系综继承部分与观察期间噪声产生部分；对扩散与跳跃过程引入单一鞅振幅同时刻画涨落与响应；通过鞅的协方差表示响应，进而导出不等式、变分原理及多元、频率域界，并应用于欠阻尼动力学与极限环。

Result: 得到了一个在任意有限时间下均可饱和的摩擦-响应TUR，它给出熵产生的变分原理；证明速度分辨电流（即使在线性高斯动力学中）会对传统TUR造成指数级强违反；对极限环导出一个弱噪声耗散-相干界，表明惯性振子只有在频率对摩擦弱敏感时才能打破过阻尼界；对过阻尼动力学与跳跃过程，该框架恢复并推广了近期涨落-响应恒等式，并指出相应不确定关系何时饱和。

Conclusion: 基于Doob鞅的框架为一般马尔可夫过程提供了统一的精确有限时间涨落-响应关系，既能推广并饱和TUR类不等式，又能识别传统TUR的失效条件，为从实验涨落与响应推断耗散提供了新工具。

Abstract: Fluctuations and responses of time-integrated observables are both experimentally measurable, and relations between them can reveal the dissipation that sustains a nonequilibrium state. Here we develop a martingale framework that gives exact finite-time fluctuation-response identities for additive observables of general Markov processes. We obtain an exact identity for the variance of an observable in terms of its Doob martingale, which splits the total variance into a part inherited from the initial ensemble and a part generated by noise during the observation. We show that, at fixed preparation, the response of the mean of the observable to any admissible perturbation is the covariance between the Doob martingale and a second martingale that represents the perturbation. For diffusions and jump processes, a single martingale amplitude determines both the fluctuations and the response of the observable. These identities yield inequalities, variational principles, and multivariate and frequency-domain bounds, whose costs are set by the corresponding physical perturbations. Applying the framework to underdamped dynamics, we obtain a friction-response thermodynamic uncertainty relation (TUR) that can be saturated at any finite time and thus gives a variational principle for the entropy production. The integrability-preserving scaling limit is obtained by adding a higher conserved charge and tuning the chain to criticality. The gap closes quadratically, so that the limit must be described by some nonrelativistic field theory rather than the relativistic sigma model. We describe the two-particle scattering matrix of this field theory.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: $q$-余切触与 $q$-接触几何为约束力学系统提供了自然且富有结构的框架，可实现局部正规形、自同构刻画与 Noether 型守恒/耗散关系。磁单极子（尤其两个单极子）的例子表明，均匀理论在无理磁荷比等情形下存在拓扑阻碍，必须推广到非均匀 $2$-接触（乃至一般非均匀 $q$-接触）结构，从而为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: $q$-Cosymplectic and $q$-contact structures equip presymplectic and precontact geometry with additional one-forms and a normalized family of Reeb vector fields. We study canonical and canonoid transformations relative to these data, with particular attention to their origin in constrained mechanical systems. In the $q$-cosymplectic and uniform $q$-contact cases, we establish local normal forms, characterize infinitesimal automorphisms, and obtain Noether-type relations between symmetries and conserved or dissipated quantities. For a one-parameter group of canonoid transformations with generator $X$, we show that $[X,X_H]$ is Hamiltonian and determine its Hamiltonian function. In the uniform $q$-contact case, simultaneous constant rescalings of the defining forms provide canonoid transformations for every Hamiltonian. The geometric constructions are illustrated by regular relativistic mass-shell constraints and by global Lagrangian descriptions of a charge in magnetic monopole fields. These examples distinguish parameters along constraint orbits from physical time and clarify the role of gauge directions. They also recover the total angular momentum of free relativistic particles and the Poincaré angular momentum of the charge--monopole system. For two monopoles, we construct a non-uniform $2$-contact structure and prove a topological obstruction to a uniform structure with the prescribed gauge generators as Reeb fields. Irrationally related magnetic charges additionally obstruct the intermediate quotient that would produce a single contact manifold. This example identifies a limitation of the uniform theory and motivates its extension to non-uniform structures.

</details>

For this class we compute the hopping coefficients in closed form, prove that the Lieb--Robinson velocity vanishes, and determine when the almost--local algebra lies in the domain of the generator. For the Landau Hamiltonian the dynamics turns out to be periodic. We then describe the ground states and the KMS states at every temperature, prove their ergodicity under magnetic translations, and recover the KMS states at positive temperature as thermodynamic limits of local Gibbs states. Finally, we relate the exact hopping operator to the Hofstadter model. These results confirm that the frame--induced local structure is a consistent setting for the perturbed and interacting magnetic dynamics to be studied next.

With the $\bar{\partial}$-steepest descent method and double scaling limit technique, we derive the long-time asymptotics of the solution in two transition regions \begin{align*}

  :=\left\{(x,t)\in\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}:
  \left| {x}/{t}-a\right|t^{2/3}<C\right\},

We conjecture that, in this limit, the normalized quantum density and its evolution at fixed scaled time $τ=c\bar n t$ are described by a canonical-momentum average of normalized periodic one-soliton GP densities, each evolving at its own velocity. We explicitly parametrize the GP family by canonical momentum and provide numerical evidence for this correspondence. The different velocities explain the spreading of the averaged density notch despite shape preservation by individual solitons. In the infinite-line limit, we derive an analytic expression for the initial averaged profile, establish an inverse-fifth-power decay of its density deficit with distance from the soliton center, and express the Fourier transform of its deviation from the background as a series of Bickley--Naylor functions $\operatorname{Ki}_3$. Yrast transition frequencies distinguish the initial dephasing time scale from the longer scale associated with finite-time averaging of fluctuations.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: In earlier work we introduced a finite-partition propagator for $\mathbb{C}_p$-valued wave functions, in which the intermediate positions are integrated against atomic measures. The iterated integral evaluates the action on the equally spaced chain between the endpoints, so each partition level is obtained by iterating an exact, tree-level block decimation of two-point lattice actions, with no integration over fluctuations. This kinematic decimation is the Fréchet derivative, at the free actions, of the variational decimation underlying classical perfect actions. Since the time step $p^n$ is small in the real norm exactly when it is large $p$-adically, refinement and coarsening (the ultraviolet and infrared directions over $\mathbb{R}$) exchange roles, and each yields one limit. Under refinement, potentials supported on $\mathbb{Z}_p$, given there by a convergent power series, and entering the slice action without the time-step weight produce an interaction that stabilizes off the diagonal after finitely many steps and converges uniformly on $\mathbb{Z}_p^2$, with sharp error $p^{-M}$ after $M$ steps, to a scale-covariant (perfect) action, governed near the diagonal by a Volkenborn integral; with the weight no limit exists. For the harmonic oscillator no refinement limit exists at fixed frequency, but under coarsening the variational flow is solved in closed form: the lattice angle is multiplied by $p$ at each step, the coupling obeys a Chebyshev recursion, no caustic occurs, and the rescaled action converges to a free action with renormalized mass $m_0\sin_p(θ_0)/θ_0$. The rescaled linearized map is diagonalizable on polynomial actions with eigenvalues $p^{2-b}$, so directions relevant over $\mathbb{R}$ are irrelevant over $\mathbb{Q}_p$ and conversely.

Abstract: We study the local eigenvalue correlations of products of independent induced symplectic Ginibre matrices in the large-dimensional limit. Our proofs are based on a unified approach involving a differential equation for the skew prekernels and their asymptotics.

Method: 作者结合量子群 U_q(sl_2) Verma 模张量积的表示论、qKZ 联络的传输算子及超几何积分技巧。核心做法是考察 m 步 qKZ 传输在固定度数子空间上的极限，利用幂映射 z↦z^m 的拉回结构与 KZ 参数缩放 m 来识别极限联络；在度数 1 情形通过对归一化超几何圆积分按角腔分类求极限，并借助 loaded 超几何积分、上三角矩阵以及右弱 Bruhat 序刻画解析延拓与幂映射的相容性。

Abstract: Over the field of complex numbers, we study root-of-unity limits of trigonometric qKZ connections associated with tensor products of $U_q(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ Verma modules and of their hypergeometric solutions. We show that this connection is the pullback of the trigonometric KZ connection under the power map $z\mapsto z^m$, with the KZ parameter rescaled by $m$, and establish formal and $\ell$-adic versions.

The limits are expressed in terms of loaded hypergeometric integrals for the trigonometric KZ equations and explicit chamber-dependent upper-triangular matrices. We establish compatibility of the resulting distinguished fundamental matrices under inward analytic continuation, governed by the right weak Bruhat order,and under power maps, including composition of root orders.

Abstract: The twinor analogue of the googly problem is addressed through an ambitwistor structure defined by null autoparallels, retaining both chiralities, sources and torsion without requiring chiral null surfaces. These constructions give a conditional auxiliary formulation; constitutive sources and gravitational prescriptions remain dynamical input.

### [71] [Where electrons conduct and where to change it: gauge-invariant sensitivities and the networks of sensitive bonds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25104)
*D A Drabold,C Ugwumadu*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文回答了"在无序固体中，局域改变会在何处改变电流"这一精确问题：Kubo-Greenwood 电导率对哈密顿量的导数具有规范不变性，可给出对任意局域改变的响应。作者将该方法应用于非晶硅，并与 SPC、KuboMap、LDOS 三种导电性分配方式对比，发现 KuboMap 与 LDOS 高度相关，而"敏感性"图则不同且迁移性较差。最敏感键形成连通簇（价带尾部为树枝状细丝，带隙深处为环状网络），它与尾部本征态和主导导电通道重叠显著，但在 300 K 热快照中基本消失，说明"电流在哪里"与"电流在哪里响应"是两个不同的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无序固体中，"电流由何处携带"与"局域改变会改变何处电流"是两个不同的问题。以往工作（如 SPC、KuboMap、LDOS）主要关注前者，而缺少一个精确、规范不变的框架来回答后者，即局域扰动对电导率的真实响应。

Method: 作者将 Kubo-Greenwood 电导率对哈密顿量求导，构造出规范不变、适用于任意哈密顿量的"敏感性"度量，用以精确给出对任意局域改变的响应。该方法被应用于非晶硅，采用最多 10000 原子的紧束缚元胞和最多 512 原子的密度泛函元胞，并与空间投影电导率（SPC）、KuboMap 和局域态密度（LDOS）三种导电性分配方式对比，还通过 300 K 热快照检验其稳定性。

Result: KuboMap 与 LDOS 高度相关（中位 Spearman 相关系数 0.97），但敏感性图不同。敏感性图能准确排序对在位能移动的响应，但对移除原子的响应排序较差，且在哈密顿量之间迁移性不如其他图。最敏感的键形成连通簇：在三个大网络中，价带尾部为 24–38 原子的树枝状细丝，带隙深处为环状网络；其相对 LDOS 加权随机选择的超出量达 48 个标准差，且仅当展宽跨越约五个能级间距时才出现。细丝与该尾部权重最大的本征态及主导导电通道之一的重叠远高于随机簇，但没有任何单一态或通道能解释它。这些网络在 300 K 热快照中基本消失。

Conclusion: "导电性在何处被携带"与"导电性在何处响应"是不同的问题：局域输运图（SPC、KuboMap、LDOS）对前者有用，但对后者作用有限。精确的敏感性分析揭示了独特的连通结构（细丝与环状网络），表明对无序固体导电性的局域调控需要专门的处理方法。

Abstract: Where is the electrical current carried in a disordered solid, and where would a local change alter it? The two questions differ. We answer the second exactly: the derivative of the Kubo-Greenwood conductivity with respect to the Hamiltonian is gauge invariant and gives the response to any local change. The construction applies to any Hamiltonian. We try it on amorphous silicon, with tight-binding cells of up to 10000 atoms and density-functional cells of up to 512 atoms, and compare it with three ways of allocating the conductivity: the space-projected conductivity (SPC), KuboMap, and the local density of states (LDOS). KuboMap follows the LDOS closely (median Spearman correlation 0.97). The sensitivity does not. It ranks the response to an on-site shift accurately and the response to removing an atom poorly, and it transfers between Hamiltonians less well than the other maps. The most sensitive bonds form connected clusters. In three large networks they are tree-like filaments of 24 to 38 atoms in the valence tail and looped networks deeper in the gap; the excess over an LDOS-weighted random selection reaches 48 standard deviations and appears only when the broadening spans about five level spacings. The filament overlaps the tail eigenstate with the most weight on it, and one of the leading conduction channels, far more than a random cluster does, but no single state or channel explains it. The networks are largely lost in thermal snapshots at 300 K. Where the conductivity is carried and where it responds are different problems: local transport maps are useful for the first and limited for the second.

</details>


### [72] [Onset of subdiffusion in dephased quasiperiodic lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26082)
*Ashlin V. Thomas,Sanchayan Banerjee,Tapan Mishra*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究准周期晶格中退相干（dephasing）对输运的影响，发现传统预期（退相干导致扩散）在具有奇异在位势或消失跃迁的晶格中会被颠覆，输运可呈亚扩散，且在最慢点（亚扩散沟）处出现尖锐特征，输运指数具有普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为退相干因破坏相位相干性而导致准周期晶格中的扩散输运，但已有观点过于简单。作者关注具有奇异在位势或消失跃迁项（本应平凡局域化粒子）的晶格，探究退相干在其中是否及如何诱发非平凡输运，从而修正对退相干输运的普遍认知。

Method: 结合解析推导与数值分析，利用“平均电阻”发散条件刻画输运行为，分析奇异在位势（对角项零点）与消失跃迁（非对角项零点）的奇异性阶数对输运指数的影响；并通过精确 Lindblad 模拟验证结论，同时提出实验实现路径。

Result: 发现在适当条件下此类晶格的输运可呈亚扩散（subdiffusive），并随准周期调制强度出现从扩散到亚扩散的转变；最慢输运并非出现在亚扩散区深处，而是在其起始点，形成尖锐的“亚扩散沟”（subdiffusive trench）；输运指数具有普适性，仅由对角（在位）或非对角（跃迁）准周期调制的奇异性（零点）阶数决定。

Conclusion: 退相干诱导扩散的传统图像在奇异准周期晶格中失效，系统可呈现由奇异性阶数普适决定的亚扩散输运，并在相变临界点出现“亚扩散沟”；精确 Lindblad 模拟证实了理论结果，并为实验观测这类非平凡输运提供了可行路线。

Abstract: Dephasing is widely expected to induce diffusion in quasiperiodic lattices due to the loss of phase coherence. However, this expectation can fail dramatically in lattices possessing singular onsite potentials or vanishing hoppings which are expected to trivially localize the particle. Remarkably, we show that the transport in such lattices can be subdiffusive under proper conditions for which the "mean resistance" of the lattice diverges. As a result, we obtain a transition from diffusive to subdiffusive transport depending on the strength of quasiperiodic modulation. Even more strikingly, the transport is slowest not deep in the subdiffusive regime but precisely at its onset, forming a sharp "subdiffusive trench". Our analytical and numerical analysis reveal that the transport exponents are universal, fixed solely by the order of singularities (zeros) of the diagonal (off-diagonal) quasiperiodic modulation. In the end, we complement our findings through exact Lindblad simulations and provide possible experimental routes to realize such non-trivial transport.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文实验演示了由四个相同樟脑椭圆转子组成的系统中出现的三模类嵌合体（three-mode chimera-like）状态，验证了反应扩散模型中三域嵌合体的理论预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌合体态是同步对称性破缺的重要体现，但三域（三模）嵌合体此前主要停留在反应扩散模型的理论预测层面，缺乏实验验证。作者希望通过物理实验系统证实其存在，并探索其通用性。

Method: 作者构建了一个由四个相同樟脑灌注椭圆转子组成的系统，转子铰接在方形底座的四个角上，置于水面。转子因马兰戈尼（Marangoni）驱动流而自推进并旋转，通过共享水界面实现化学耦合，从而产生三种不同的集体动力学状态。

Result: 实验观察到三种集体动力学状态：同步态、反同步态和非相干态，即在四个相同单元中自发分裂为相干与不相干组，验证了三模类嵌合体状态的存在。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了反应扩散模型中三域嵌合体的最新理论工作，为非线性动力学提供了新见解，并表明这种新型嵌合体可能在一大类物理系统中被观察到。

Abstract: Chimera states are a remarkable manifestation of symmetry breaking, wherein identical units spontaneously split into coherent and incoherent groups. The term "chimera" itself is inspired by the mythological creature having parts of different animals, reflecting the coexistence of distinct dynamical behaviors within a single system. Here, we experimentally demonstrate the existence of a three-mode chimera-like state in a system of four identical camphor-infused elliptical rotors, each pivoted at a corner of a square base. When placed on a water surface, the rotors self-propel and rotate due to Marangoni-driven flows. Chemical coupling mediated through the shared water interface gives rise to three distinct collective dynamical states, namely a synchronized one, an antisynchronized one and an incoherent one. Our results validate recent theoretical work about three-domain chimeras in reaction-diffusion models, providing new insights into nonlinear dynamics and suggesting that this new type of chimera may be observed in a broad class of physical systems.

</details>

Conclusion: M-理论局部模型的非平凡拓扑通过几何 bordism 构造的 SymTFT 接口，能够自然地引入额外拓扑扇区并与局域 QFT 耦合，从而调和冻结理论中电、磁对称性的失配问题。

Abstract: M-theory local models may exhibit torsional cycles and other non-trivial topology which interpolate between quantum field theory sectors localized at singularities and the asymptotic regions of the local model. We show that such structures give rise, in standard decoupling limits, to additional topological sectors which can couple non-trivially to the localized QFT. From a symmetry topological field theory (SymTFT) perspective, the physical boundary conditions associated to such local models are modified. We give a construction of SymTFT interfaces from geometric bordisms interpolating between the local singularity and the asymptotic boundary. We apply this construction to the circle compactification of a frozen $D_5$ singularity, identifying a topological interface between the $\mathbb{Z}_4$ SymTFT of the full theory and the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ SymTFT of its local $\mathfrak{su}(2)$ gauge sector. We show that the resulting coupling between the gauge theory and an additional topological quantum field theory accounts for the apparent mismatch between electric and magnetic symmetries of the frozen theory.

</details>

Result: 该综述系统地整理了进入Scherk–Schwarz紧致化研究所需的知识链条与工具，覆盖了从初等场论到弦理论的递进性内容，并明确不讨论唯象应用。

Conclusion: 本文为希望进入Scherk–Schwarz紧致化领域的读者提供了一条结构化的学习路径，充当文献阅读前的导引；唯象应用被有意排除在外，以聚焦于理论与形式工具本身。

Abstract: This review is written with a simple question in mind: what does one need to know to enter the literature on Scherk-Schwarz compactifications? It is organized as a sequence of questions that a graduate student might naturally ask, together with the concepts, calculations, and formal tools needed to work through the literature. The discussion starts at an elementary field-theory level and gradually develops toward more advanced aspects of supergravity, orbifolds, quantum effects, and string theory. Phenomenological applications are deliberately left aside.

</details>

Abstract: We consider what the entanglement between two regions of a critical system can reveal about its symmetries, including non-invertible ones. For an on-site symmetry this computes the overlap of the reduced density matrix with its image under the symmetry acting on one interval, and in general it defines an observable that depends only on the cross-ratio. As the intervals approach each other, the observable vanishes at a rate set by the lightest operator on which the line can end, and its monodromy is fixed by the spins of such operators. Abstract: We derive the soft divergences of $\hat{N}=-i\log\hat{S}$ in the Worldline Quantum Field Theory (WQFT) formalism, focusing on poles due to single massive-massive (Coulomb) and massive-massless (tail) graviton exchanges. Our derivation is inspired by the Faddeev-Kulish procedure, switching the interaction picture to one with an infrared-finite $\hat{N}$-matrix. Soft divergences emerging from the worldline are correlators of Wilson lines. As confirmation of this structure, we demonstrate explicitly how divergences of inclusive observables including the momentum impulse $Δp_i^μ$ and spin kick $ΔS_i^μ$ cancel, and we reproduce the infrared divergence of the one-loop scattering waveform. Using the Magnus series representation of $\hat{N}$ and Murua coefficients, we then calculate the Magnusian $N=\langle0|\hat{N}|0\rangle$ at fourth post-Minkowskian (4PM) order, including linear-in-spin corrections. For aligned spins, $-\partial N/\partial L$ reproduces the full scattering angle through 3PM order; at 4PM order, $N$ develops a soft divergence as predicted by our soft factorisation. Using the Feynman $i0^+$ prescription, we also derive an infrared-finite 4PM Magnusian which compactly encodes existing results for the conservative momentum impulse and spin kick via nested iterations of Poisson brackets.

</details>

Abstract: The one-loop dilatation operator of a superconformal planar gauge theory can be written as a sum of local generators. The algebra these generators satisfy is a powerful organising principle for the spectrum. For SU(2)-like bosonic subsectors we show that this algebra can be read off directly from Feynman diagrams, which we identify as a natural graphical language for it. For N=4 super Yang-Mills this simply reproduces the Temperley-Lieb (TL) algebra of the XXX spin chain. After orbifolding and marginally deforming to N=2 superconformal quiver theories, the same recipe yields dynamical generalisations of the TL algebra. In these theories, the faces of the diagrams are coloured according to the quiver nodes, and separated by domain walls corresponding to bifundamental fields. There are two inequivalent SU(2)-like subsectors, which lead to two different TL algebras. One sector is built from the two bifundamental scalars and (in the simplest case) gives a dynamical TL algebra with a colour-dependent loop fugacity in its quadratic relation. The other sector is built from one bifundamental and one adjoint scalar, and (always) gives a novel algebra, in which the cubic TL relation becomes dynamical. In this latter case, we show that each generator of this new algebra is related to a standard XXZ TL generator by a colour-dependent twist. This twist is local and not an algebra homomorphism, and can be written as a dynamical Drinfeld twist. In both sectors, the TL structure provides a natural graphical approach to the spectral problem, as well as clarifying the structure of the irreducible representations of these chains. Finally, we give a preliminary description of the loop models associated to these algebras.

Abstract: We establish a direct connection between entanglement and complexity through spacetime density matrices. For stationary pure states and complementary regions at different times, spacetime replica moments encode the return amplitude of a replica-defect quench. This representation defines an associated state spread complexity, which we call Krylov-Rényi complexity. It can equivalently be obtained by using a replica-defect excitation of the replicated background state as the seed and evolving it under the original replicated Hamiltonian. We further construct Krylov-von Neumann complexity as a probe limit from first-order replica data. Timelike entanglement and complexity provide complementary information at a fixed time, while the full time dependence of the timelike entropy determines the Krylov dynamics and hence the complexity. The replica construction fixes the seed and its evolution through the background state, subsystem geometry, and regulator, without an independently chosen probe operator. Like entanglement entropy, the resulting complexity is defined nonperturbatively and probes universal properties and dynamical information about the underlying theory.

The Taub-NUT index agrees with the Maxwell result. For Eguchi-Hanson, the gauge-field contribution to the axial anomaly depends explicitly on the ModMax parameter when expressed in terms of the solution parameters. For asymptotically anti-de Sitter solutions of New Massive Gravity with Brown-Henneaux boundary conditions, a term linear in the extrinsic curvature reproduces the holographic stress tensor of the auxiliary-field formulation. At the degenerate point, relaxed boundary conditions admit an additional constrained Fefferman-Graham coefficient. A quadratic extrinsic counterterm cancels the residual power divergence. Abstract: We construct twists of spinning particles in the classical BV formalism by giving constant backgrounds to the bosonic ghosts of local worldline supersymmetry, in analogy with Costello and Li's twist of supergravity, which gives a nonzero background to the ghost of local spacetime supersymmetry. We represent the superghosts on polynomial states and gauge selected worldline R symmetries, imposing the shifted R constraints absolutely and the unshifted ones relatively. On flat targets, the N=2 particle, its N=4 extension on a complex target and an N=8 particle with two fermion flavors give the de Rham, Dolbeault and polynomial linear BCOV complexes. The last two are the linearized field content of the open and closed B-model target-space theories. Pairings and interactions require target-space input beyond the reduced worldline differentials.

</details>

Abstract: We study the holographic bulk dual of 2D CFTs evolved by $sl^{(q)}(2,R)$-deformed Hamiltonians. Working in the Hamiltonian-adapted frame, in which the $sl^{(q)}(2,R)$-deformed effective Floquet Hamiltonian generates time translations, we construct the dual Bañados geometries for the three distinct classes of dynamics: the heating phase, the non-heating phase and the phase boundary. Specifically, we show that in the heating phase, the bulk patch dual to the region between two neighbouring fixed points has a time-dependent causal horizon and asymptotes to a static AdS Rindler geometry at late times, while the non-heating phase has no horizon. We discuss how the late-time behaviour of the entanglement entropy, particularly its linear growth in the heating phase, can be understood from the bulk in two different ways: in global AdS, from the portion of the geodesic behind the horizons, and in a quotient description, from an effective shockwave produced by a boosted conical defect. [1] by reproducing the exact CFT expression of the OTOC from the bulk and explaining why it exhibits an exponential growth {\it only} when the operators lie on the side of the two fixed points.

</details>

Conclusion: 作者成功建立了辐射子-引力子质量比与 Ricci 标量奇异行为之间的关联，并为幂次型和指数型弯曲模型导出了 R_{11} 的绝对上界。指数模型的上界 R_{11}<1 及第二模式上界 R_{21}≲1.374 均与幺正求和规则自洽，为理解弯曲额外维模型的谱结构和唯象约束提供了重要参考。

We consider warped models with an admissible singular behavior in the infrared: i.e.~models with power-like ($\sim \barφ^{2n}$) and with exponential ($\sim e^{2ν\barφ}$) bulk potentials, where $\barφ$ is the stabilizing bulk field. We find a correlation between $R_{11}$ and the singular behavior of the Ricci scalar, which allows to establish an absolute upper bound on the former. For the power-like models, the maximum value of $R_{11}$ increases with $n$. In the limit $n\to\infty$, the power-like model reproduces the exponential model and reaches the same upper bound as the latter. For the exponential model, we derive analytically the bound $R_{11}<1$, with the largest value approached for the case $ν\to 1^+$ (where $ν=1$ corresponds to the linear dilaton model) and consistent with unitarity sum rules. For exponential models we also obtain the absolute upper bound on the second radion mode ratio $R_{21}=m_{r_2}/m_{h_1}\lesssim 1.374$. This value is reached for $ν=2$ when the IR brane approaches the singularity. For this configuration, we verify that the scalar contribution satisfies the unitarity sum rule when the complete radion Kaluza-Klein tower is included.

Abstract: We investigate mutual information and logarithmic negativity in fermionic Schwinger pair production in parallel electric and magnetic fields. We also consider an initially entangled two-particle state and evaluate the correlations for six bipartitions. The physical Landau-level structure is treated explicitly by distinguishing modes with the same oscillator index from the spin representatives of a common physical Landau level. For matched higher levels, the pairwise redistribution relations retain the electric-only form, with the magnetic-field dependence entering through the Landau-level pair-production probabilities. When the two particle groups occupy different physical levels, the cross particle--antiparticle correlations become unequal, and we derive analytic relations characterizing this asymmetry. We further obtain the local logarithmic negativity from the ordinary partial transpose in a fixed occupation-space basis. Finally, we extend the analysis to a Sauter-type electric field in a constant magnetic background and examine the dependence of the pairwise correlations on the field parameters and Landau-level structure.
