Abstract: Chiral fermions in $d$ dimensions arise naturally as boundary modes of a massive fermion in $d+1$ dimensions subject to a local boundary condition. We present a formulation in terms of second-order differential operators that is completely equivalent to the original formulation in the continuum. Its natural lattice discretization generates Wilson's term and avoids fermion doubling. Our lattice formulation closely resembles the boundary-fermion construction of Shamir, and provides some insight into the case of a general Wilson parameter.

### [98] [Holographic Tessellations Tensor Networks $\{p,q\}$ from Entangled Quantum Tetrahedron in Loop Quantum Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25993)
*Deden M. Akbar,Freddy P. Zen,Jusak S. Kosasih*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过镶嵌全息张量网络，将圈量子引力（LQG）中的自旋网络态与体–边界对偶进一步联系起来。作者利用四面体间的量子纠缠构建全息张量网络，把四价intertwiner空间和带SU(2)holonomy的Bell态映射到双曲{p,q}镶嵌上的有效张量。对{5,4}镶嵌，六个四面体组装成一个有五个网络腿和一个逻辑指标的有效元胞，并在微观转移映射满行秩条件下构造等距嵌入，得到正比于完美张量的有效张量。该构造推广到p=2n−1族，局部维度支持绝对最大纠缠态。在满足径向取向条件的有限网络上，适当归一化产生等距体–边界映射。对两边满足等距条件的割，边界熵分解为链接贡献与体熵之和，实现离散化的量子Ryu–Takayanagi结构。通过后选择型非等距编码处理黑洞内部，并在Haar统计假设下分析平均重叠保持与熵分支竞争，从而解释Page曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进一步探索圈量子引力中自旋网络态与体–边界对偶（AdS/CFT）之间的关系，建立一个从LQG几何自由度出发构造全息码的显式桥梁，并澄清将其与LQG几何面积、量子极值面位置及黑洞蒸发相联系的所需条件。

Method: 基于LQG中四面体间量子纠缠构造全息张量网络，将四价intertwiner空间及带SU(2)holonomy的Bell态映射到双曲{p,q}镶嵌的有效张量。对{5,4}镶嵌，组装六个四面体为一个有效元胞（五网络腿、一逻辑指标），在微观转移映射满行秩条件下用右逆和核空间上的补全构造等距嵌入，得到正比于完美张量的有效张量；推广至p=2n−1族（局部维度支持绝对最大纠缠态）。在径向取向的有限网络上以适当归一化构造等距体–边界映射，并通过后选择型非等距编码处理黑洞内部，附加Haar统计假设分析平均性质。

Result: 对满足等距条件的割，边界熵分解为链接贡献Σ_ℓ log(2j_ℓ+1)与体熵之和，给出量子Ryu–Takayanagi结构的离散实现；在Haar统计下框架保持平均重叠，并呈现竞争熵分支下的Page曲线行为。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了LQG几何自由度与全息码之间的构造性联系，明确了将其与LQG几何面积、量子极值面位置及黑洞蒸发联系所需的条件，并实现了离散版Ryu–Takayanagi结构及Page曲线的解释。

Abstract: The relationship between spin network states in loop quantum gravity and bulk--boundary duality is further explored using the tessellated holographic tensor network. We develop a holographic tensor network construction based on quantum entanglement between tetrahedra in loop quantum gravity (LQG). This construction relates four-valent intertwiner spaces and Bell states dressed with $SU(2)$ holonomies to effective tensors on hyperbolic $\{p,q\}$ tessellations. For the $\{5,4\}$ tessellation, six tetrahedra are assembled into a single effective cell with five network legs and one logical index. Provided that the microscopic transfer map has full row rank, a right inverse and a completion on the kernel space are employed to construct an isometric embedding, yielding an effective tensor proportional to a perfect tensor. The construction is generalized to the $p=2n-1$ family, with local dimensions supporting absolutely maximally entangled states. On finite networks satisfying a radial orientation condition, an appropriate normalization produces an isometric bulk--boundary map. For cuts satisfying the isometry condition on both sides, the boundary entropy decomposes into a link contribution $\sum_{\ell}\log(2j_{\ell}+1)$ and the bulk entropy, thereby providing a discrete realization of the quantum Ryu--Takayanagi structure. Applications to the black hole interior are formulated via postselection-based non-isometric encoding. Assuming additional Haar statistics, this framework is used to analyze the preservation of overlaps on average and the competition between entropy branches underlying the Page Curve. These results establish a constructive link between the geometric degrees of freedom of LQG and holographic codes, while clarifying the conditions required to connect them to geometric area in LQG, the location of the quantum extremal surface, and evaporation of black hole.

</details>


### [99] [Worldsheet Effective Theory of Abelian-Higgs Cosmic Strings beyond Nambu--Goto](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26013)
*Sergio Alameda-Calvo,Josu C. Aurrekoetxea,Jose J. Blanco-Pillado,Alberto García Martín-Caro,J. M. Queiruga*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从临界耦合的Abelian-Higgs模型出发，推导出宇宙弦内部模式（有质量标量形状模与有质量矢量模）与横向Goldstone模耦合的协变世界面理论，并通过格点场论数值模拟验证；对矢量模做对偶化后揭示出标量模耦合内禀曲率、赝标量模耦合法丛曲率的几何区别，从而解释了格点模拟中不同的参数不稳定性，并预言碰撞横波偏振对内部模式激发的控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越Nambu--Goto近似，建立微观弦结构与非线性世界面动力学之间的定量联系，统一描述宇宙弦核心内被束缚的长寿命激发（内部模式）与描述弦运动的横向Goldstone模式之间的能量交换机制。

Method: 直接从临界耦合的Abelian-Higgs模型推导协变世界面理论，获得所有耦合系数均由微观理论确定的耦合动力学；通过对矢量模进行对偶化区分标量与赝标量的几何耦合；并用格点场论数值模拟验证所得作用量。

Result: 得到扩展Nambu--Goto描述的作用量，包含一个有质量标量形状模和一个有质量矢量模；对偶化后显示标量形状模耦合内禀世界面曲率，而赝标量耦合法丛曲率，这一几何区别解释了格点模拟中观察到的不同参数不稳定性。

Conclusion: 该框架将微观弦结构与非线性世界面动力学相连接，为超越Nambu--Goto近似的宇宙弦研究提供了可预测的理论工具，并预言了碰撞横波的相对偏振对两个内部模式激发的调控作用。

Abstract: Cosmic strings can support long-lived excitations confined to their cores, opening channels for energy exchange with the transverse Goldstone modes that describe the string's motion. We derive a covariant worldsheet theory of their coupled dynamics directly from the Abelian-Higgs model at critical coupling, with all interaction coefficients fixed by the microscopic theory. The resulting action, which we validate against numerical lattice field theory simulations, extends the Nambu--Goto description to include a massive scalar shape mode and a massive vector mode. Dualizing the vector reveals a geometric distinction: the shape scalar mode couples to intrinsic worldsheet curvature, while the pseudoscalar couples to normal bundle curvature. These interactions explain the distinct parametric instabilities observed in lattice simulations and predict how the relative polarization of colliding transverse waves controls the excitation of the two internal modes. Our results connect microscopic string structure to the nonlinear worldsheet dynamics, providing a predictive framework for cosmic strings beyond the Nambu--Goto approximation.

</details>


### [100] [Equilibrium relativistic spin magnetohydrodynamics from chiral kinetic theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26087)
*Gopal Mistry,Amaresh Jaiswal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从一阶手征动力学理论出发，建立了无质量手征费米子相对论平衡自旋磁流体力学的一致微观框架，推导出熵流、平衡分布、自旋极化及二阶磁涡旋响应，并与场论结果精确吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相对论平衡自旋磁流体力学缺乏从微观动力学理论出发、且满足热力学一致性的统一推导；需要通过手征动力学理论将量子动力学、自旋极化、磁化与反常输运直接联系起来。

Method: 以协变Wigner函数为出发点，将手征动力学方程重构为磁位移质量壳上的纯流方程，同时纳入磁矩能量与Berry曲率相空间测度；由此建立动力学H定理与全局平衡下散度为零的熵流；构造任意自旋标架下的平衡分布并导出相应流、能动张量、自旋张量、熵流与标架无关的自旋极化；再由热力学一致性确定二阶磁涡旋响应。

Result: 得到相对论Barnett磁化、与温度和化学势无关的反常电荷密度，以及转动诱导的密度修正，且这些结果精确复现场论结果；同时导出了粒子与反粒子间的磁分裂效应。

Conclusion: 该框架为相对论平衡自旋磁流体力学提供了统一的微观基础，并在量子动力学理论、自旋极化、磁化与反常输运之间建立了直接联系。

Abstract: We develop a microscopic and thermodynamically consistent framework for relativistic equilibrium spin magnetohydrodynamics of massless chiral fermions, directly from first-order chiral kinetic theory. Starting from the covariant Wigner function, we recast the chiral kinetic equation as a pure streaming equation on a magnetically shifted mass shell, incorporating both the magnetic-moment energy and the Berry-curvature phase-space measure. This formulation yields a kinetic $H$-theorem and an entropy current with vanishing divergence in global equilibrium. We construct the equilibrium distribution for an arbitrary spin frame and derive the associated currents, energy--momentum tensor, spin tensor, entropy current, and frame-independent spin polarization, including the magnetic splitting between particles and antiparticles. Thermodynamic consistency further determines the second-order magnetovortical responses, revealing a relativistic Barnett magnetization, a temperature- and chemical-potential-independent anomalous charge density, and rotation-induced density corrections that reproduce exact field-theory results. Our framework provides a unified microscopic foundation for relativistic equilibrium spin magnetohydrodynamics and establishes a direct link between quantum kinetic theory, spin polarization, magnetization, and anomalous transport.

</details>


### [101] [Cardinal RG=GR: Phase-Space Cardinality and the Cosmological Constant and Hierarchy Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26170)
*Laurent Freidel,Jerzy Kowalski-Glikman,Robert G. Leigh,Djordje Minic*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种替代性的量子化方案：将量子理论限制在一个有限的相空间可及区域，并用无量纲基数 $\mathcal N$ 表征该区域，而非独立设定时空标度与动量标度。由此，de Sitter 时空的引力作用量驻值条件可改写为对动量标度的驻值条件（称为 "Cardinal RG=GR"），并由此导出宇宙学常数正比于 $1/\sqrt{\mathcal N}$、证明其辐射稳定性，同时阐明层级问题的解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准有效场论方法通过在紫外标度以上积分掉涨落来构造低能理论，这会加剧宇宙学常数问题与层级问题。作者希望探索一种不依赖独立紫外截断的替代方案，从而更自然地解释宇宙学常数的微小性与质量的辐射稳定性。

Method: 作者提出将量子理论限制在一个有限的可及相空间区域，该区域由无量纲基数 $\mathcal N$ 刻画，且在固定 $\mathcal N$ 时时空标度与动量标度相互关联。对于欧氏 de Sitter 空间，引力作用量的驻值条件被改写为对动量标度的驻值条件（Cardinal RG=GR）。进而计算了 $\mathcal N$ 与 Gibbons-Hawking 熵的对应关系，并将同一方案应用于一个简单相互作用标量场模型检验质量参数的辐射稳定性，同时建立了在包含引力数据的极限下恢复到通常量子场论与重整化群结果的精确条件。

Result: 研究表明：$\mathcal N$ 与 de Sitter 空间的 Gibbons-Hawking 熵直接相关；宇宙学常数正比于 $1/\sqrt{\mathcal N}$，且具有辐射稳定性（即不因圈图修正产生大的二次发散）；同一方案用于简单交互标量场模型时，也验证了质量参数的辐射稳定性，从而为层级问题提供了解释。

Conclusion: 通过以基数 $\mathcal N$ 限定相空间而非独立设定紫外截断，可以统一处理引力与标量场问题，得到辐射稳定的宇宙学常数与质量参数，缓解宇宙学常数问题与层级问题，并在包含引力数据的特定极限下与标准量子场论及重整化群结果一致。该框架为理解量子引力中的标度问题提供了新视角。

Abstract: The effective field theory approach constructs the low-energy theory by integrating out fluctuations above an ultraviolet scale which raises the cosmological constant and hierarchy problems. We investigate an alternative prescription in which the quantum theory is restricted to a finite accessible region of phase space, characterized by a dimensionless cardinality $\mathcal N$, rather than by independently prescribed spacetime and momentum scales. At fixed cardinality these scales are related. For Euclidean de Sitter space, the stationarity condition for the gravitational action can then be written as a stationarity condition with respect to the momentum scale, a relation which we call Cardinal RG=GR. We demonstrate the relation between $\mathcal N$ and the Gibbons-Hawking entropy of de Sitter space, compute the cosmological constant proportional to ${1}/{\sqrt{\mathcal N}}$, show that it is radiatively stable and apply the same prescription to demonstrate the radiative stability of the mass parameter for a simple interacting scalar field model, thereby illuminating the resolution of the hierarchy problem. We also establish a precise limit, involving gravitational data, in which the conventional results of Quantum Field Theory and the Renormalization Group are recovered.

</details>


### [102] [Full-Color Two-loop Four-point Form Factor in N=4 SYM with Global CK-duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26194)
*Zhiming Cai,Yuanhong Guo,Gang Yang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在 N=4 超杨-米尔斯理论中，利用色-运动学对偶与幺正性切割，完整确定应力张量超多重态的两圈四点 MHV 形状因子，并给出全色（含非平面）圈积分被积函数及有限余项的新结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两圈四点 MHV 形状因子的全色（full-color）结果在高圈散射振幅与形状因子研究中长期缺失，尤其非平面贡献与相关红外发散结构、劈裂函数和软流等物理量尚未被完整确定；本文旨在填补这一空白，并检验色-运动学对偶在高圈、离壳且全色情形下的适用性。

Method: 作者采用色-运动学（CK）对偶与幺正性切割方法，构造出显式满足全局离壳 CK 对偶、且不含显式 μ² 依赖的一圈与两圈被积函数；随后通过积分-约化（IBP）将其约化到均匀超越度（uniform-transcendentality）的母积分，并以五边形函数（pentagon functions）表示结果，最后做全色红外极点抵消并提取有限余项。

Result: 得到的结果为纯有理数乘以简单运动学前因子，明显体现最大超越度（maximal transcendentality）；验证了全色红外极点抵消，并首次给出两圈三重共线劈裂函数（triple-collinear splitting function）与双重软流（double-soft current）的非平面新结果。

Conclusion: 本文完整确定了两圈四点 MHV 应力张量超多重态形状因子的全色结果，证明色-运动学对偶可推广到高圈且无显式 μ² 依赖的离壳情形；所得最大超越度结构、红外极点抵消及新非平面劈裂函数与软流结果，为更高圈与更复杂的全色形状因子计算提供了基准与方法支撑。

Abstract: We determine the full-color two-loop four-point MHV form factor of the stress-tensor supermultiplet in N=4 super-Yang-Mills theory. Using color-kinematics (CK) duality and unitarity cuts, we construct complete one- and two-loop integrands manifesting global off-shell CK duality without explicit $μ^2$ dependence. Integration-by-parts reduction to uniform-transcendentality master integrals yields purely rational numbers multiplying simple kinematic prefactors, manifesting maximal transcendentality. Expressing the form factor in terms of pentagon functions, we verify full-color infrared-pole cancellation, extract the finite remainder, and obtain new non-planar results for the two-loop triple-collinear splitting function and double-soft current.

</details>


### [103] [Exact Spherical Branes and Deformations: BMN, IKKT and $\mathcal{N}=2^\ast$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26278)
*Cameron Gibson*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文对球面 Dp-膜（1≤p≤5）的 BPS 方程给出统一解析解及其 II 型超引力提升，并通过一个可积化的变量代换解析计算全息 Wilson 圈，同时将该方法推广至 D0/D(-1) 膜、极化 IKKT 的一球静电解以及 N=2* SYM 的对偶，系统性地解析求解了一系列此前只能数值处理的全息 BPS 系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前球面 Dp-膜的全息对偶（即 S^{p+1} 上极大超对称 Yang-Mills）中，BPS 方程及全息可观测量大多依赖数值方法，缺少统一的解析结果，且 p=0、p=-1 等退化情形及极化 IKKT、N=2* 等扩展体系缺乏闭合形式解；作者希望借助使方程可积化的变量代换，统一解析地解决这些问题。

Method: 作者找到一个使球面 Dp-膜 BPS 方程可积的变量代换，对 1≤p≤5 给出统一解析解并构造对应的 II 型超引力背景；利用同一变量代换解析计算全息 Wilson 圈并得到 UV 渐近行为。将该代换加以适当修改推广到 p=0（得到一参数族 D0 膜解，并分析 IR 条件导致的积分常数奇异极限）与 p=-1（证明所得欧氏提升不满足正则性条件）。另外，通过椭圆不变量给出极化 IKKT 一球静电解的显式表示，并解析求解 N=2* SYM 在 S^4 上全息对偶的 BPS 方程，构造 IIB 提升并计算全息 Wilson 圈。

Result: 成功给出 1≤p≤5 球面 Dp-膜的统一解析 BPS 解及闭式的 II 型提升；全息 Wilson 圈的普适解析结果及此前仅数值确定的 UV 渐近行为被解析复现；得到一参数族 D0 膜解并揭示 IR 条件导致积分常数奇异；D(-1) 膜解被证明不满足正则性；极化 IKKT 的静电解可用椭圆不变量显式表示，从而能以之表达多极矩和全息可观测量；N=2* SYM 的 S^4 对偶 BPS 方程获解析解，并给出 IIB 提升与全息 Wilson 圈。

Conclusion: 本文通过关键变量代换实现了多类球面对称全息 BPS 系统的解析求解，统一了 Dp-膜（含退化情形）、极化 IKKT 与 N=2* 等体系，显著推进了对这些全息对偶中 BPS 解与可观测量的解析理解，并为后续研究闭合形式解提供了系统方法。

Abstract: We present a unified analytic solution to the BPS equations of spherical Dp-branes for $1\leq p \leq 5$ and give the corresponding type II uplifts in closed form. These are holographically dual to maximally supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory on $S^{p+1}$. The change of variables that makes the BPS equations integrable also allows us to analytically calculate a universal result for the holographic Wilson loop and obtain UV asymptotic behaviour previously determined numerically. The same change of variables extends naturally, with suitable modifications, to the cases $p=0$ and $p=-1$. We obtain a 1-parameter family of D0-brane solutions and show how the IR conditions lead to a singular limit of the integration constant. We also solve the D(-1)-brane BPS equations and show the resulting Euclidean uplift fails regularity conditions. Separately, we obtain an explicit representation of the one-ball electrostatic solution of polarised IKKT, which lets us express multipole moments and holographic observables using elliptic invariants. Finally, we analytically solve the BPS equations of the holographic dual of $\mathcal{N}=2^\ast$ supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory on $S^4$, present the corresponding type IIB uplift and calculate the holographic Wilson loop.

</details>


### [104] [All-Order Linearized Hydrodynamics of Charged Holographic Plasmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26279)
*Karthik R.,Shamant Shreedhar Dixit,K. M. Ajith,Kartheek Hegde,Shreyas Punacha,A. Naveena Kumara*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究有限密度全息等离子体中的线性化全阶输运，在五维Einstein-Maxwell-Chern-Simons理论框架下保留频率和动量的完整依赖，揭示了不同输运通道的时间响应层级、有限建立时间以及近极端条件下记忆的持久化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究往往局限于低阶流体力学展开或特定极限，忽略了频率与动量的完整依赖，无法完整刻画有限密度强耦合等离子体中非局域输运与弛豫的时间组织方式，尤其是有无建立时间、记忆效应如何随密度和温度变化等问题。

Method: 在五维Einstein-Maxwell-Chern-Simons全息对偶框架下，对有限密度等离子体做线性化处理，保留流体/引力方程中完整的频率和动量依赖，推导广义剪切、扩散、高阶梯度以及手征涡旋输运函数及其实时记忆核。

Result: 不同通道呈现出清晰的时间响应层级：主导的剪切与扩散扇区即时响应，而高阶梯度扇区须经历有限建立时间后才发展起来；手征涡旋响应虽在流体力学导数展开中为一阶，却也表现出有限建立时间，说明导数阶并不单独决定输运的时间组织。趋近极端极限时，多个通道的长时记忆愈发持久。

Conclusion: 有限密度显著重塑了强耦合全息等离子体中的非局域输运与弛豫结构，时间响应并非由导数阶简单决定，高阶与手征涡旋通道的有限建立时间以及近极端下持久的长时记忆构成其核心特征。

Abstract: We investigate linearized all-order transport in a finite-density holographic plasma described by five-dimensional Einstein-Maxwell-Chern-Simons theory. By retaining the full frequency- and momentum-dependence of the fluid/gravity equations, we determine generalized shear, diffusion, higher-gradient, and chiral-vortical transport functions together with their real-time memory kernels. The different channels exhibit a clear hierarchy of temporal response: the leading shear and diffusion sectors respond promptly, whereas the higher-gradient sectors develop only after a finite buildup time. Remarkably, the chiral-vortical response also shows a finite buildup time despite entering at first order in the hydrodynamic derivative expansion, demonstrating that derivative order does not by itself determine the temporal organization of transport. Toward the near-extremal regime, long-time memory becomes increasingly persistent across several channels. These results reveal how finite density reshapes nonlocal transport and relaxation in strongly coupled holographic plasmas.

</details>


### [105] [Black holes and 2-group symmetry from blackfolds in minimal supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26287)
*Jay Armas,Giorgos Batzios,Pedro Vicente Marto,Chiara Toldo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用 blackfold 方法和广义对称性框架，研究五维最小超引力的黑洞相图：推导了 2-群守恒律并构造二维 2-群流体，匹配到非极端黑洞弦的热力学，进而微扰构造了多种新黑洞解（黑环、土星、双环、双环）及 AdS₅ 中的极端黑环分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 五维最小超引力的黑洞相图具有丰富的拓扑结构（黑环、黑土星、双环等），但缺乏一个能统一组织这些解并处理其微扰与极端极限的系统性框架。作者希望借助 blackfold 有效理论与广义对称性（2-群）的语言，从守恒律和热力学匹配出发，提供一个可系统构造和分类新黑洞解的途径。

Method: 作者从超引力耦合探针源出发，推导 2-群的（非）守恒律，构造二维 2-群流体的平衡作用量并计算相应整体守恒荷；随后将该流体的热力学与非极端（非规范）理论中一般非极端黑洞弦的热力学相匹配，用对称不变量组织 blackfold 有效理论及其解；在此基础上对非极端非规范扇区进行微扰构造新解，并取极端极限；最后将同一框架推广到 AdS₅ 构造极端黑环。

Result: 在非极端非规范扇区微扰构造出新的黑洞环、土星、双环和双环解，其中包括一个具有七个独立参数的黑土星；通过取极端极限得到若干极端分支（部分此前未探索、部分符合预期）以及近似已知超对称族的 BPS 分支；此外在 AdS₅ 中识别出三个正规的极端黑环分支，被认为是有望获得精确完备化的候选者。

Conclusion: blackfold 方法结合 2-群广义对称性为系统研究五维最小超引力的黑洞相图提供了有力工具，成功统一匹配了非极端黑洞弦热力学并构造、分类了大量新黑洞解及其极端/BPS 极限，为后续寻找 AdS₅ 极端黑环的精确解奠定了基础。

Abstract: We investigate the black hole phase diagram of five-dimensional minimal supergravity using the blackfold approach and the framework of generalized symmetries. Starting from supergravity coupled to probe sources, we derive the 2-group (non-)conservation laws, construct the equilibrium action for a two-dimensional 2-group fluid, and compute the associated global conserved charges. We then match the thermodynamics of the general non-extremal black string in the ungauged theory to that of this fluid, organizing the blackfold effective theory and its solutions in terms of symmetry invariants. In the non-extremal ungauged sector, we perturbatively construct new black rings, Saturns, di-rings, and bi-rings, including a black Saturn with seven independent parameters. Taking appropriate extremal limits, we identify several extremal branches, some previously unexplored and others anticipated, as well as BPS branches that approximate known supersymmetric families. Finally, we apply the same framework to construct extremal black rings in AdS$_5$, identifying three regular branches that are promising candidates for an exact completion.

</details>


### [106] [Inhomogeneous Cutoff Regularization of Loops in Cosmological Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26495)
*Jacopo Fumagalli,Keisuke Inomata*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视宇宙学关联函数圈修正计算中的截断正规化方法，提出截断应相对于局域背景几何的固定物理尺度来定义，并论证这种非均匀截断对保持超视界相关函数的时间不变性及满足一致性关系至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统截断正规化通常以全局背景定义固定物理尺度，但在存在超视界扰动时，不同局域块（separate universe）的物理尺度应各自独立确定。作者旨在揭示这种非均匀截断对圈级宇宙学关联函数（特别是软外模情形）的影响，以及它对晚期时间不变性和一致性关系的必要性。

Method: 作者在 separate universe 图像下，将固定物理尺度的截断定义为随局域背景几何变化，并将此截断在长波背景上展开，得到一系列可图释为长模插入到相应圈收缩上的正规子诱导贡献。该截断方案保持残余空间伸缩与保体积各向异性重标度不变性。将其应用于单圈标量与张量功率谱以及挤压极限单圈标量双谱的计算。

Result: 对超视界单圈标量与张量功率谱，非均匀截断引入的额外修正恰好抵消普通图中所出现的紫外边界项，从而在截断正规化框架下确立了两种功率谱的晚期时间不变性。对挤压极限单圈标量双谱，额外截断贡献对在有限截断下满足一致性关系不可或缺。文章还讨论了非均匀截断在单圈双谱晚期时间不变性中的作用。

Conclusion: 截断正规化必须依照局域背景几何（而非全局背景）定义固定物理尺度；由此产生的非均匀截断修正对抵消紫外边界项、保证超视界功率谱的晚期时间不变性以及满足挤压极限一致性关系均具有本质作用，对软外模的圈级关联函数尤为重要。

Abstract: We revisit the use of cutoff regularization in computing loop corrections to cosmological correlators. We argue that a cutoff corresponding to a fixed physical scale must be defined with respect to the local background geometry. In the presence of superhorizon perturbations, this is naturally implemented within the separate universe picture, where different local patches are regulated at the same physical scale. Expanding this cutoff in the long-wavelength background generates a series of regulator-induced contributions to loop amplitudes, which admit a diagrammatic interpretation as long-mode insertions on the corresponding loop contractions. Our cutoff prescription preserves residual spatial dilatations and volume-preserving anisotropic rescalings. It is therefore particularly relevant for loop correlators with soft external modes. We illustrate the mechanism by computing the contributions to the one-loop scalar and tensor power spectra on superhorizon scales. In both cases, the additional corrections induced by the inhomogeneous cutoff cancel the ultraviolet boundary terms arising in the ordinary diagrams. These calculations establish, within cutoff regularization, the late-time constancy of both power spectra and clarify its physical origin. We then investigate the squeezed one-loop scalar bispectrum and show that the additional cutoff contributions are essential for satisfying the consistency relation at finite cutoff. Finally, we discuss the role of the inhomogeneous cutoff in the late-time constancy of the one-loop bispectrum.

</details>


### [107] [UV Bounds on Inflationary Parity Violation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26594)
*Alexander Cassem,Mark P. Hertzberg*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究宇宙大尺度上宇称违反的可能性，重点考察由暴胀动力学陈-西蒙斯算子产生的宇称奇四点函数的可探测性，并发现现有张量-标量-标量三点函数界限强烈限制了可探测宇称奇四点函数信号的产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期对四点物质关联函数的分析提示宇宙大尺度可能存在宇称违反，且暴胀期间的动力学陈-西蒙斯算子（广义相对论的一个主要有效场论修正）被认为可能产生可探测的宇称违反信号。作者旨在检验该可能性在现实紫外完备（如费米子和弦论例子）中是否成立。

Method: 在包含费米子和弦论实例的现实紫外完备框架下，考察动力学陈-西蒙斯算子产生宇称奇四点函数的可行性，并利用现有的张量-标量-标量（TSS）三点函数界限对该过程进行约束分析。

Result: 现有TSS三点函数界限强烈限制了产生可探测宇称奇四点函数的可能性。当张标比接近当前观测极限时，可探测性与这些紫外完备的有效域互相矛盾或处于临界状态；只有在张标比足够小时才可能保留可探测信号，但代价是TSS顶点需要极大的增强。

Conclusion: 虽然未来对大尺度宇称违反的搜寻值得进行，但在自洽的有效理论中产生可探测信号是高度非平凡的。

Abstract: A question of fundamental interest is whether parity is violated on large scales in the Universe. This possibility has been motivated by recent analyses of the four-point matter correlation function. It has also been suggested that detectable parity violation could be generated during inflation by the dynamical Chern-Simons operator, a leading effective field theory correction to general relativity. In this work, we examine this possibility in realistic UV completions, including fermionic and string-theoretic examples. We show that existing bounds on the tensor-scalar-scalar (TSS) bispectrum strongly constrain the possibility of generating a detectable parity-odd trispectrum. For tensor-to-scalar ratios near current observational limits, detectability is incompatible with, or pushes against, the regime of validity of these UV completions. A detectable signal can remain possible for a sufficiently small tensor-to-scalar ratio, but only at the cost of an extremely large enhancement of the TSS vertex. Thus, while a future search for large scale parity violation is worth pursuing, generating a detectable signal within a consistent effective theory is highly nontrivial.

</details>


### [108] [Equivariant iterated Eisenstein integrals for congruence subgroups of level 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26597)
*Emiel Claasen,Mehregan Doroudiani,Claude Duhr,Axel Kleinschmidt,Franca Lippert,Oliver Schlotterer,Yoann Sohnle*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究全纯Eisenstein级数的原函数（迭代Eisenstein积分）在级2同余子群下的模变换性质，通过将迭代Eisenstein积分与复共轭及二级分圆多重zeta值结合，构造出非全纯模形式（称为等变迭代Eisenstein积分）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SL_2(Z)及其同余子群的全纯Eisenstein级数的模性质并不能直接传递给其原函数（迭代积分）。在级N同余子群下，迭代Eisenstein积分的变换会引入含有级N分圆多重zeta值等非齐次项。作者的动机是寻找一种方式，通过引入分圆多重zeta值来"修正"这些非齐次项，使所得对象成为真正的非全纯模形式，从而将Brown在SL_2(Z)情形的构造推广到级2同余子群。

Method: 作者在生成级数层面进行构造，这些生成级数取值于一个无穷维（pro-幂单）李群，并紧密遵循Brown在SL_2(Z)情形的类似构造。具体做法是将任意迭代深度的级2同余子群迭代Eisenstein积分与其复共轭及（单值）级2分圆多重zeta值相结合。所需插入的分圆多重zeta值由"级2 zeta生成元"控制，这些生成元将生成级数对应的（pro-幂零）李代数正规化，作者系统确定了这些生成元的李括号关系。

Result: 作者为Γ(2)和Γ_0(2)构造了所谓的等变迭代Eisenstein积分，证明了它们定义非全纯模形式；并系统确定了级2 zeta生成元的李括号关系。在限制到模权2的Eisenstein级数时，得到的结果为亏格0模曲线Γ(2)\H上的单值多重多对数（single-valued multiple polylogarithms）。

Conclusion: 本文成功将Brown关于SL_2(Z)的等变迭代Eisenstein积分构造推广到级2同余子群Γ(2)和Γ_0(2)，通过引入级2分圆多重zeta值抵消了变换中的非齐次项，从而得到非全纯模形式，并揭示了其与单值多重多对数的联系。

Abstract: The modular properties of holomorphic Eisenstein series for $\mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbb{Z})$ and its congruence subgroups do not carry over to their primitives. Instead, the transformation of iterated Eisenstein integrals under congruence subgroups of level $N$ introduces inhomogeneous terms comprising (among others) cyclotomic multiple zeta values of level $N$. We combine iterated Eisenstein integrals for the congruence subgroups of level $N=2$ of arbitrary iteration depth with their complex conjugates and (single-valued) level-2 cyclotomic multiple zeta values such that they define non-holomorphic modular forms. Our construction of these so-called equivariant iterated Eisenstein integrals for $Γ(2)$ and $Γ_0(2)$ proceeds at the level of generating series that take values in an infinite-dimensional (pro-unipotent) Lie group and closely follow Brown's analogous construction in the $\mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbb{Z})$ case. The required insertions of cyclotomic multiple zeta values are controlled by zeta generators for level 2 that normalise the corresponding (pro-nilpotent) Lie algebra of the generating series and whose Lie-bracket relations we determine systematically. Upon restriction to Eisenstein series of modular weight~2, we recover single-valued multiple polylogarithms on the genus 0 modular curve $Γ(2)\backslash \mathbb{H}$.

</details>


### [109] [Entropy Products and the Extremal Limit That Never Comes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26628)
*Kevin Goldstein,Vishnu Jejjala,Suresh Nampuri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在Einstein–Maxwell–scalar理论中构造了两类渐近平坦的正则黑洞分支，证明双视界黑洞的熵乘积不必由电荷和耦合常数唯一确定，可以高于或低于极值熵的平方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于双视界黑洞存在一个预期：视界熵的乘积应由电荷和耦合常数固定（如同弦论中某些模不变性或多视界解所暗示）。作者旨在说明这一性质并非普遍成立，通过正则性条件可以打破该约束。

Method: 在Einstein–Maxwell–scalar理论中显式构造渐近平坦的静态球对称正则黑洞分支，利用正则性条件选取视界处的标量场值；允许不同分支共享相同的电荷、耦合常数与渐近标量值，并使熵乘积分别位于极值熵平方之上或之下；通过局部存在性证明确立解的存在，并分析质量与渐近标量变化时熵乘积的恒定性与温度行为。

Result: 构造出的分支中熵乘积随质量和渐近标量变化保持恒定；两视界面积始终分离，不存在极值极限；温度仅在质量发散处趋于零；并给出了局部存在性证明。

Conclusion: 正则黑洞双视界的熵乘积不必由电荷和耦合常数固定，正则性条件仅约束视界标量取值，因而可存在共享电荷与耦合但熵乘积不同的多分支解，说明此类"熵乘积固定"的性质并非普遍规律。

Abstract: Two regular black hole horizons need not have an entropy product fixed by the charges and couplings. In Einstein--Maxwell--scalar theory, we construct asymptotically flat branches with products above or below the squared extremal entropy. Regularity selects their horizon scalar values, and different branches can share the charges, couplings, and asymptotic scalar. Each product stays constant as mass and asymptotic scalar vary. The horizon areas stay apart and no extremal limit is reached: the temperature vanishes only where the mass diverges. We prove local existence.

</details>
